pukaḻpeṟṟa putukkaviñarkaḷ


					kaviñar muruku cuntaram

13 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu

patippurimai aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimaic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉaittup patippurimaikaḷaiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattaip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭaiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēvaiyillai.

﻿muṉṉurai



putukkavitaipaṟṟik kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷ iraṇṭu:

(1) putukkavitai purivatillai.

(2) putukkavitaiyil purintu koḷḷa eṉṉa irukkiṟatu?



口

paṇṭaik kālattil kaviñaṉukku ellām terintirukka vēṇṭum; avaṉ cakalakalā vallavaṉ.

iḷaṅkō kaviñar maṭṭumallar; icai mētai; nāṭakāciriyar; vāṉanūl vallunar; navamaṇikaḷiṉ kuṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷai uṇarntavar.

maikkēl ēñcalō ōviyar; ciṟpi; kaviñar.

liyaṉārṭō ṭāviṉci ōviyar; ciṟpi ; kaṭṭiṭa mētai; poṟiñar; iyaṟpiyal aṟiñar; kaviñar.

aṟiviyal mētaiyāṉa kalīliyō kaviñarum kūṭa.

ketē mākaviñar; icaimētai; nāṭakāciriyar; araciyal aṟiñar. tamatu amaiccar poṟuppiliruntu viṭuppu eṭuttuk koṇṭu pāricu nakaram ceṉṟu ōviyam payiṉṟār; tāvaraviyal (Botany) paṟṟiyum oru nūl eḻutiyirukkiṟār.

ṣēkspiyar ulakam aṟinta tellām aṟintavar. nam nāṭṭilum ‘kalviyil periyavaṉ kampaṉ’;

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟiviyal poruḷiyal, araciyal, uḷaviyal, tattuvaviyal ākiyavaṟṟil ēṟpaṭṭa vaḷarcciyiṉ kāraṇamāka innilai māṟat toṭaṅkiyatu.

﻿ovvoru kalaiyilum ciṟapput tuṟaikaḷ (Specialization) tōṉṟikkiḷaittaṉa. ōr iyaṟpiyalvātiyiṉ moḻi maṟṟōr iyaṟpiyalvātiyālum, oru tuṟaiyaic cārnta ciṟpi, ōviyar allatu icai mētaiyiṉ nuṭpam, avvat tuṟaiyaic cārnta kalaiñarkaḷālum maṭṭumē purintu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ippātippu kavitaiyilum taṉit taṉmai vāynta, ciṟappup pirivukaḷait tōṟṟuvittatu.

urainaṭaiyiliruntu taṉṉai vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvataṟkākak, kavitai ivvaḷavu kālamākattāṉ aṇintirunta ceḻumai, iruṇmai mayakkum mantiram ākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai iḻakka vēṇṭi nēriṭṭatu.

camakāla makkaḷōṭu pēcuvataṟkākavum, araciyal uṇarvukaḷai veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkākavum, ṭiraiṭaṉ, pōp, pairaṉ, ṭeṉṉicaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ kavitai moḻiyiṉ cila paṇpukaḷait tiyākam ceytaṉar. ṭaṉ , kāleriṭj, piḷēk, hāpkiṉs pōṉṟa kaviñarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkeṉṟu oru kuṟukalāṉa vaḻiyait tērnteṭuttuk koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ vācakarkaḷiṉ aḷavaik kuṟukkik koṇṭaṉar.

āṉāl, kavitait tuṟaiyil uṇmaiyāṉa puraṭciyai uṇṭākkik kavitaipputumai (Modernism in Poetry) yait tōṟṟuvittavaṉ pireñcu nāṭṭaic cērnta patiṉāṟu vayatu iḷaiñaṉ rempō. avaṉ kavitai eḻutiyatu mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ maṭṭumē. am mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷil avaṉ eḻutiya kavitaikaḷ airōppiyak kavitaiyulakaiyē talai kīḻākap puraṭṭiviṭṭaṉa. eṉṟālum, rempō eṉṉum utaya cūriyaṉukku viṭiveḷḷiyāka muṉṉāl niṉṟavar pireñcu makākavi pōtalēr eṉpataiyum kuṟippiṭṭāka vēṇṭum.

pōtalēriṉ ‘mānakarak koḷkai’yai appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koṇṭatōṭu, viṭalaip paruvak kaṉavukaḷaiyum uṇarvukaḷaiyum mikattulliyamākat taṉ paṭaippukaḷil mutaṉ mutalāka veḷippaṭuttukiṟāṉ rempō.

inta kōpakkāra iḷaiñaṉ (angry young man) paḻamaiyōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa camayam, araciyal, potu oḻukkam ākiyavaṟṟai maṟutalittatōṭu, kavitai moḻiyaiyum etirttup puraṭci ceytāṉ. kavitaic coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa poruḷaiyē māṟṟiṉāṉ. urai naṭaiyilēyē uyarnta kavitaikaḷai eḻutiṉāṉ.

kaṭavuḷ matam ākiyavaṟṟil nampikkai vaikkāta rempō, “oru kaviñaṉ taṉṉaiyē ñāṉiyākkik koṇṭu, taṉ āṉmāvait tēṭi cōtittuṇarntu ataip paṇpaṭuttik koḷvataṉ mūlam ‘pāva vimōcaṉam’ peṟalām” eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟāṉ.

﻿rempōviṉ tākkam pireñcuk kavitai mītum, maṟṟa airōppiyak kavitaikaḷ mītum aḷaviṭamuṭiyāta aḷavu iruntatu. ēṟkeṉavē pireñcu nāṭṭil kuṟiyīṭṭiyakkam kāl koṇṭiruntatu, mallārmē, verlēṉ pōṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭukkaviñarkaḷ paḻañ ciṟappiyac cimmācaṉattiliruntu kavitaiyaik kīḻē iṟakki, ataṉ vaṭivattil putiya cōtaṉai muyaṟcikaḷ mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. uḷaviyal aṟiñarāṉa ḵprāyṭiṉ āyvukaḷ kalaiyilakkiyat tuṟaiyil puraṭcikaramāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvittaṉa ‘puṟa meymmaiyiya’ (Surrealism) iyakkam tōṉṟiyatu.

paṇṭaiya marapukaḷaip puṟakkaṇittal, uḷḷattaiccutantiramāka vaittukkoḷḷutal, kaṉavukaḷil mūḻkiyiruttal, mikaikkaṟpaṉaikaḷil īṭupaṭutal, akakkāṭcikaḷiṉ tuṇaiyāl puṟavulakaip purintu koṇṭu vāḻviṉ aṭippaṭai uṇmaikaḷai aṟitalyāvum, puṟameymmai iyakkattiṉ paṇpukaḷ ākum. ivviyakkam 1924-il āntar pireṭaṉ eṉpavarāl muṟaiyākat tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭālum, itaṉ aṭippaṭaik kūṟukaḷ ellām rempōviṉ paṭaippukaḷil muḻumaiyākap poruntiyirukkak kāṇalām. eṉavē puṟa meymmai iyakka muṉṉōṭi rempōtāṉ eṉpatil entavita aiyappāṭum illai.

inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa āṉmīkak kaviñaṉ eṉṟum, mikac ciṟanta taṉṉuṇarccik kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ eṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭupavaṉ jermaṉ nāṭṭaic cērnta reyṉarmēriyā rilk. ivaṉ paḻamaiyil nampikkaiyaṟṟavaṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya cintaṉai vaḷarcciyai, kalai nuṇukkac cemmaiyil īṭupaṭṭirunta kuṟiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷ, veṟittaṉamākac cūṉyattaip piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟa rempō ākiyōriṉ vaḷarccikku aṭutta vaḷarcciyākak koḷḷalām. taṉṉaiyē aḻittukkoḷḷum ulakap perumpōrukku etirāṉa makkaḷuṇarcci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻantaiyāka ivaṉaik kuṟippiṭalām. ivaṉuṭaiya kavitaikaḷil iruṇmaik kūṟu mika atikam.

“eṉ pāṭalkaḷ iṟukkamum curukkamum mikkavai. ivaṟṟai ‘aṟintu koḷḷutal’ eṉpataiviṭa ‘uḷḷuṇarvāl uṇarntu koḷḷutalē’ paṭippōrāl iyalum” eṉṟu rilkkē ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya uṇmaiyāṉa ulakam kaṇṇukkut teriyāta meypporuḷ ulakamē (World of Spirit). avvulakiṉ uṇmaiyai uṇarntu tuykka, poyyāṉa ipparup poruḷ ulakil, aṉpum maṉitat taṉmaiyumē vaḻi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. im muyaṟciyil tēvataikaḷaiyum tuṇaikkaḻaikkiṟāṉ.

itē ‘āṉmīkat tēṭal’ , pēkay, ṭi. es. eliyaṭ, ṭi. ec. lāraṉs, ṭilāṉ tāmas, rāparṭ lōval ākiyōriṉ paṭaippukkaḷil vevvēṟu vaṭivam koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku nērmāṟākac camatarmak kaviñarkaḷ oru pūlōka ﻿corkkattait tam kaṟpaṉaiyil paṭaikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. mārkciyak kaviñarkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavar jermaṉ nāṭṭaic cērnta perṭōlṭ prekṭ. rilktaṉimaṉitaṉiṉ āṉma taricaṉattaip pāṭiyavar. prekṭ makkaṭcamutāyattiṉ ēkkaṅkaḷaip piratipalitta cīrtiruttavāti, rilk cīmāṭṭikaḷiṉ maṭiyilamarntu āṉmāvaip paṟṟic cintittavar; prekṭ teruvōrttilamarntu ēḻmaiyaip paṟṟic cintittavar. rilkkiṉ kavitai aṟivai mayakkum putirkkavitai (mystic poetry). prekṭiṉ kavitai aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyai āyvu ceyyum naṭappiyak kavitai (Functional poetry) itaip putiya meymmaiyiyam (New Realism) eṉṟum collalām.

taṟperumai, tattuvārttam, pirapañca rakaciyam, aṭimaṉak kaṟpaṉai eṉpavaṟṟaip pāṭu poruḷākak koṇṭuvarum kavitaikaṭku etirāṉatu naṭappiyakkavitai. nērppaṭum uṇmaikaḷai uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyākat taramāṉa kavitaikaḷil aṟivārnta cintaṉaiyōṭu veḷippaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṭappiyaṟ kaviñarkaḷ virumpiṉar.

prekṭ kavitaiyiṉ ellāc caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaitteṟintu viṭṭu eḻutiṉār; tamatu karuttukkaḷai makkaḷukku vaṉmaiyākak kūṟum kaṇṭippāṉa āciriyarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. muṉṉōkkāḷar iyakkam (Futurist movement) ruciyāvilum, ittāliyilum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil paraviyatu, pḷāk, ehrṉ, pōrk, māyakōsski ākiyōr muṉṉōkkiyattai ātaritta muṉṉōṭikkaviñarkaḷ. ittāli nāṭṭaic cērnta ṭi’aṉṉaṉsiyō, māriṉeṭṭi ākiya iru kaviñarkaḷum ḵpāciliyattiṉ tattuvat tantaikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. muṉṉōkkiyamum, ḵpācisamum taṉimaṉita cutantirattai (individualism) muṟṟāka veṟuppavai.

viḷāṭimir māyakōvski ruciyappotuvuṭaimaik koḷkaiyāl kavarappaṭṭu, ataṉ puraṭcik kuralāka olittavar. avar eḻutiya kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitai, vālṭ viṭmaṉ, appōliṉar, māriṉeṭṭi ākiyōraip piṉapaṟṟip puraṭciyiṉ tiṭīr vēkattōṭum, kuttūcik kiṇṭalōṭum eḻutappaṭṭavai: ālait toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi pāṭappaṭṭu, ruciya makkaḷiṭaiyil atika viḷamparam peṟṟavai.

inta nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaviñarkaḷuḷ, atikamāka ulaka ilakkiya vātikaḷiṉ kavaṉattaik kavarntavarkaḷ iruvar; avarkaḷ ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭum, esrā pavuṇṭum.

ṭi.es. eliyaṭ kiṟittava camayattiṉpāl atika īṭupāṭu koṇṭa āṉmīkak kaviñar. vāḻum kālattilēyē peruṅ kaviñarākavum ilakkiya mētaiyākavum matikkappaṭṭu elllārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭṭavar, ivaratu paṭaippiluḷḷa iruṇmaiyiṉ kāraṇamāka aruvak kaviñar (invisible poet ﻿eṉṟu ilakkiya vātikaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭupavar. āḻntakaṉṟa perumpulamaiyum, marapilakkiyap payiṟciyum mikkavar. ivar paṭaippāṉa ‘pāḻ nilam’ (Waste Land) oppaṟṟa kuṟiyīṭṭuk kāppiyamākavum, aṅkatamākavum karutappaṭṭu, ulakiṉ uyarnta paricāṉa nōpel paricaiyum peṟṟatu, mutal ulakap pōrukkup piṉ airōppāvil ēṟpaṭṭa naṉmīka vaṟaṭciyaiyum, ataṉ viḷaivāka makkaṭ camutāyattil ēṟpaṭṭa cīrkulaivaiyum ‘pāḻnilam’ vivarikkiṟatu.

ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭiṉ camakālattavarum naṇparumāṉa esrā lūmis pavuṇṭ ciṟanta kaviñar; paṉmoḻi vallunar; palkalai aṟiñar; cīrtiruttavāti. jappāṉiya haikkū kavitaikaḷiṉ ciṟappai ulakaṟiyacceytavar. peruṅkaviñar yēṭs, eliyaṭ pōṉṟōriṉ kavitaikaḷil tamatu kattarik kōlai nīṭṭic cemmaippaṭuttiyavar. yārum neruṅkuvataṟku añciya, piṭivātamāṉa muraṭṭu ilakkiyamētai. kavitaittuṟaiyil ‘paṭima iyakkattait’ tōṟṟuvittavar. ḵpācisattai ātarittup piracāram ceytavar; mucōliṉiyiṉ naṇpar. kaṇṭapaṭi maṉampōṉa pōkkil eḻutappaṭṭa putukkavitai ivarāl ceṟivum, iṟukkamum, cemmaiyum nuṭpamum, vēlaippāṭum mikkatāka māṟiyatu.

spāṉiṣ moḻikkaviñarkaḷāṉa lārkāvum, pāplō neruṭāvum kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷuṭaiya paṭaippukkaḷ pireñcu, āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṭaippiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavai. mutal ulakappōr muṭintapiṟaku airōppiya nāṭukaḷiliruntu kiḷampiya muṉṉōkkiyam, ṭāṭāyiyam eṉṟa puraṭci alaikaḷ resiṭeṉciyāvil irunta spāṉiya ilakkiya vātikaḷiṭattilum pātippukaḷai uṇṭākkiṉa. āṉāl lārkā enta ilakkiyak kaṭciyilum īṭupāṭu kāṭṭavillai. marapilum, putumaiyilum kāṇappaṭṭa nalla amcaṅkaḷ yāvum, avaṉaiyum aṟiyāmal lārkāviṉ paṭaippukkaḷil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. lārkā putukkaviñaṉākavum, atēcamayattil marapōṭu kūṭiya kirāmiyappāṭal pāṭum kaviñaṉākavum (Folk Poet) viḷaṅkiyatu viyappiṟkuriyatu.

pāplō neruṭā cilināṭṭuk kaviñaṉ. prekṭaippōl araciyalvāti; māyakōvskiyaippōl potuvuṭaimaik kaṭciyiṉ tīvira uṟuppiṉaṉāka iruntatōṭu, avaṉaip pōla pāṭṭāḷikaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil paṭikkum pēccuk kavitaikaḷai (spoken poetry) eḻutiyavaṉ; kiḻakku nāṭukaḷilum, airōppāvilum cilināṭṭiṉ araciyal tūtarāka nīṇṭa kālam paṇiyāṟṟiyavaṉ; cilināṭṭu araciyalil mukkiya paṅku vakittavaṉ. araciyal veḷippāṭṭiyamum, naṭappiyamum ivaṉ kavitaik koḷkaikaḷ. tāntēyaiyum milṭaṉaiyum eppaṭit teyvīkak kōṭpāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkkamuṭiyātō, hyūkōvaiyum, viṭmaṉaiyum eppaṭi makkaḷāṭcik kōṭpāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkka muṭiyātō, ﻿atupōl pāplō neruṭāvai araciyal, ēḻmai, nīti, camattuvam ākiya kōṭpāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkka muṭiyātu, ataṉāltāṉ neruṭāvai naintupōṉa ulaka apalaikaḷiṉ oṭṭu mottamāṉa āttirak kuralākavum, otukkappaṭṭa teṉṉamerikka ēḻai nāṭukaḷiṉ puraṭcic caṅkanātamākavum kēṭka muṭikiṟatu. potuvuṭaimaik kaṭciyaic cārnta kaviñarkaḷuḷ mutaṉ mutalāka nōpel paricu peṟṟavaṉ neruṭā.

irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērnta āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷuḷ, eliyaṭṭukku aṭuttāṟpōlak kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ, ṭapiḷyū. ec. āṭaṉum, ṭailāṉtāmacum āvar. āṭaṉ tīvira mārkciyavātiyāka iruntu, iruppiyal tattuvattil nuḻaintu iṟutiyil āṉmīkavātiyāka māṟiyavar. naṭappu ulakiliruntu vilakiyirunta mantirat taṉṉiyakkattaiyum (magical automatism) kuṟiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷiṉ moḻip puraṭciyaiyum, (linguístic revolution) uṅkiyalōṭu iṇaikkum kavitai muyaṟciyil ṭailāṉ tāmas īṭupaṭṭaṉar.

“kaviñaṉ eṭuttiyampiyākavum āṟutal kūṟuvōṉākavum, tīrkkatariciyākavum iruppataiviṭat taṉ conta aṉupavaṅkaḷaiyē kavitaiyāka veḷippaṭutta vēṇṭum; ataṟku varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattukku avaṉ tirumpa vēṇṭum. immuyaṟciyiṉ mūlam maṉitaṉukkuḷ kuṭiyirukkum mirukattaiyum teyvattaiyum aṟintu koḷḷuvatōṭu, varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa aṭimaṉattiṉ toṭarcciyaiyum aṟintu kalaiyaip putuppikka muṭiyum”

eṉṟu tāmas kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itu mīṇṭum iyaṟkaikkut tirumputal (return back to nature) eṉṉum kucōviṉ koḷkaiyaic cārntatu.

irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iyantiranākarikattāl cōrvuṟṟa rāparṭ krēvs eṉṟa kaviñar veṇ tēvatai (White Goddess) eṉṟa nūlukku eḻutiya muṉṉuraiyil kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu uḷḷam naintu eḻutukiṟār:

"kavitaiyiṉ ceyalpāṭu eṉpatu kalait tēvataiyiṉ karuṇaivēṭṭal, nam vēṇṭutalukkēṟpak kalait tēvatai veḷippaṭuttum uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷ nammaip paravacappaṭuttiyum, naṭuṅkac ceytum aṉupavaṅkaḷāka māṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum kavitaiyiṉ ceyalpāṭum payaṉum appaṭiyē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟālum, aṇuku muṟai māṟiyirukkiṟatu. paṇṭaināḷil iyaṟkaiyiṉ eccarikkaiyai uṇarntu maṉitaṉ, tāṉ vāḻum uyiriṉattōṭu icaintum, kuṭumpattil peṇṇiṉ viruppaṅkaḷai matittuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭum vāḻntāṉ. ippōtu anta iyaṟkaiyiṉ eccarikkaiyai matikkāmal tattuvam, aṟiviyal, toḻil ākiya tāṟumāṟāṉa

﻿āyvukaḷiṉāl vīṭṭait talaikīḻākap puraṭṭik kuṭumpa vāḻkkaiyaic cīrkulaittuk koṇṭāṉ.

“iṉṟaiya nākarikattil kavitaiyiṉ talaiyāya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ matippiḻantu pōyiṉa. pāmpum ciṅkamum vallūṟum vaṭṭak kāṭcik koṭṭakaikkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. erutukaḷum, karaṭikaḷum, mīṉkaḷum kūṇṭukkuḷ aṭaikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. vēkakkutiraikaḷum vēṭṭai nāykaḷum pantaya araṅkiṟkukkoṇṭucellappaṭṭaṉa. tūya kāṭukaḷellām aḻikkappaṭṭu aṟuppu ālaikku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. kātal nilavu, erintu taṇinta kōḷākiviṭṭatu. peṇkaḷ eṭupiṭikaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉar. paṇam, uṇmaiyait tavira ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum, uṇmaik kaviñaṉait tavira ellāraiyum vilaikku vāṅkum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkiṟatu”

mēlē ituvarai nāṉ kūṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷiliruntu, kālap pōkkil kavitaikkalai peṟṟa vaḷarcciyum, māṟutalum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷaivākappeṟṟa taṉittaṉmaikaḷum viḷaṅkum. ittaṉittaṉmaikaḷaiyum avaṟṟaic cārntu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa kavitaik koḷkaikaḷaiyum kavitai iyakkaṅkaḷaiyum purintu koṇṭāltāṉ putukkavitaiyaip purintu koṇṭu cuvaikka muṭiyum.

kavitai vaḷarntu koṇṭē irukkiṟatu. ikkaṇip poṟiyukattil kaviñaṉiṉ cintaṉaikaḷ ceyaṟkaik kōḷil ēṟi aṇṭattil pavaṉi varukiṉṟaṉa. putaṉil toṭaṅkavirukkum putu vāḻvaip paṟṟik kaviñaṉ cintikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭāṉ. kavitai oru toṭar viḷaivu.

aṭuttup ‘putuk kavitaiyil purintu koḷḷa eṉṉa irukkiṟatu?’ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrukkum, cila viḷakkaṅkaḷaik kūṟa virumpukiṟēṉ. marapuk kavitaikaḷil ‘veḷḷaippāṭṭu’ iruppatu pōl putuk kavitaikaḷilum veḷḷaippāṭṭu uṇṭu. iṉṟu tamiḻil veḷiyākum putuk kavitaikaḷil nūṟṟukkut toṇṇūṟu, kavitaikaḷē alla. avai alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa urainaṭaiyum (decorated prose), tuṇukkukaḷumtāṉ. putuk kavitaikkuriya paṇpukaḷōṭu veḷivarum kavitaikaḷaip paṭikkum pōtu, avaṟṟiliruntu nām purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu ērāḷamāka irukkum.

nilavu-

vēlikkuḷ aṭaikkappaṭṭatu;

eṉ kātalum maṭintatu.





ikkātal varikaḷiṉ avalaccuvai, nam uḷḷattil muḷḷākat taikkiṟatu. itu oru spāṉiya nāṭṭup pāṭal. itaippaṭitta lārkā paravacappaṭṭu “nāṭṭup puṟa makkaḷ virumpippāṭum inta varikaḷil putaintu kiṭakkum putir mika ﻿eḷimaiyāṉatu; cuvaiyāṉatu; tūymaiyāṉatu. ipputirc cuvai, pukaḻpeṟṟa mēṭṭarliṅk nāṭakattilum kiṭaiyātu” eṉṟu viyantu pārāṭṭukiṟāṉ.

icai

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu

itayattiṉ veḷivaḷarcci





rilkkiṉ inta varikaḷaippaṟṟi muṭivillāmal cintittuk koṇṭē irukkalām.

ruciyakkaviñar sveṭṭēvā oru kavitai nūlai eḻuti muṭittavuṭaṉ kaviñar pāsṭar nākkiṟkuk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu oru kaṭitam eḻutukiṟāḷ:

“nāṉ mikavum kaḷaittuviṭṭēṉ; āciris tuṇṭu tuṇṭāka veṭṭi noṟukki eṟiyappaṭṭatu pōl nāṉum uṇarkiṟēṉ ...camayattūtar tāmasiṉ viralkaḷ, puṇkaḷait toṭṭup pārttatu pōl nāṉum oru kavitaikkum maṟṟoru kavitaikkum iṭaiyil tīṇṭippārkkiṟēṉ.”</poem>

itu urainaṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭa kaṭitam. itil kūṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷaip purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl, ekiptiyat toṉmamum, kiṟittuva viviliyamum paṭittirukka vēṇṭum.

esrā pavuṇṭiṉ kavitaippūṭṭait tiṟavukōliṉ (Key book) tuṇaiyillāmal tiṟakkamuṭiyātu. avar kavitaikaḷaip purintu koḷḷap palamoḻiyaṟivum, paṉmukappaṭṭa ilakkiya varalāṟṟup pulamaiyum, kirēkka itikācat teḷivum peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum; cīṉa jappāṉiyak kavitaip pōkkum, kaṉḵpūciyat tattuvamum, ḵpācicamum purintirukka vēṇṭum. pērakarāti (Encyclopedia) maṟṟum paṉmoḻic ciṟṟakarātikaḷiṉ tuṇaiyum vēṇṭum. pavuṇṭiṉ camakāla naṇparkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam avar koṇṭirunta naṭpu, antaraṅkam yāvum terintirukka vēṇṭum. ivvaḷavum pōtātu; icaiyaṟivum, ciṟpa ōviyak kalaiyaṟivum vēṇṭum.”

putuk kavitaiyil purintu koḷḷa

evvaḷavō irukkiṟatu.





kavitaiyiṉ tōṟṟam paṟṟip paṇṭai nāḷ toṭṭup palvēṟu karuttukkaḷ palvēṟu kaviñarkaḷālum aṟiñarkaḷālum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kirēkka ñāṉi cākraṭṭīs.

“kaviñarkaḷ cāmiyāṭiyaip pōṉṟavarkaḷ. cāmiyāṭi cila camayaṅkaḷil maruḷ vantu aruḷ vākkuk kūṟuvatu pōla, ivarkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil āvēcappaṭṭu kavitaikaḷai veḷippaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ.”

eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ﻿kavitai āvēcam peṟuvataṟkākac cila kaviñarkaḷ matuvaiyum maṟṟa pōtaip poruḷkaḷaiyum nāṭuvatuṇṭu. pireñcuk kaviñar pōtalēr,

“kañcāp pōtaiyiṉ tuvakkattil vaḻakkattiṟku māṟāṉa vaṇṇattiṉ ātikkam namakkup pulappaṭum, kaṇṇaip paṟikkum oḷiyuṭampōṭu kāṭci tarum mōkiṉip peṇkaḷ, nīlavāṉaiviṭat teḷivāṉa āḻamāṉa viḻikaḷāl nammai uṟṟup pārppārkaḷ. appōtu ēṟpaṭum. iṉampuriyāta kṣaṉa nēra maṉa nilaikaḷil nam vāḻkkaiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷum, teḷivu cuḻivukaḷum, aṟputaṅkaḷum nam kaṇ muṉ paḷicciṭum. nam kaṇṇukku mutaṉmutalil entap poruḷ teṉpaṭukiṟatō, atuvē nam aṟivai naṭattic cellum kuṟiyīṭāka amaiyum. teḷinta nīr nilaikaḷum, nilaik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum, kaṉavulakattai namakkut tiṟantu viṭum; cōkamayamāṉa oru pēriṉ pattil nam uḷḷam āḻntu, ellaiyaṟṟa kāṭcikaḷil tiḷaikkum”

eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

uḷḷam tīviramāka uṇarccivacappaṭum pōtu, kavitai tāṉāka ūṟṟeṭuttuk karaipuraṇṭu varuvatāka āṅkilak kaviñar vōrṭs vort kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ikkaruttai ottuk koṇṭatu pōl, pireñcuk kaviñar rempōvum kavitait tōṟṟam paṟṟik kīḻkkaṇṭa karuttai veḷiyiṭukiṟār:

“kaviñaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu kūṭātu. kavitai kaviñaṉuḷḷattil tāṉākak karukkoṇṭu, kaṟpaṉai vaṭivam peṟṟup pakkuvamaṭaintu poṅki varavēṇṭum. kaviñaṉ taṉatu uḷḷattil karuttukkaḷ malarntu varutalaik kaṇṭu cuvaittu atai appaṭiyē aṉumatikka vēṇṭum.”

potuvāka, rempōvukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya puṟameymmaiyiyalār ellārum kavitai paṟṟiya ik koḷkaiyai ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. putitāka aṭimaṉak kōṭpāṭu ivarkaḷ karuttukkaḷōṭu cērkiṉṟatu.

potuvuṭaimaik koḷkaiyāḷaṉum, muṉṉōkkiya vātiyumāṉa māyakōvski, kavitait tōṟṟam paṟṟic cuvaiyāṉa karuttoṉṟaik kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu veḷiyiṭukiṟār.

“nāṉ eṉ kaikaḷai vīci vārttaikaḷ ētumiṉṟi muṇumuṇutta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ; eṉ muṇu muṇuppukku iṭaiyūṟu varāta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ. eṉ naṭaiyil vēkam kūṭumpōtu, nāṉ vēkamāka muṇumuṇukkiṟēṉ.



﻿ “ippaṭiyē taṉṉaittāṉē cetukkik koṇṭu, kavitaikku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa cantam pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampukiṟatu. avvāṟu kiḷampum anta ōcaiyiliruntu coṟkaḷ vaṭivam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

“pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampum antac canta ōcai eṅkiruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟu yārukkut teriyum? atu eṉakkuḷ tirumpat tirumpat tōṉṟum ōr oli; oru tālāṭṭu; eṉṉuḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum tōṉṟum ētō oṉṟukku nāṉ koṭukkum oli vaṭivam. anta olikaḷai muyaṉṟu iyakkutalum, oṉṟaic cuṟṟi anta olikaḷai oḻuṅkupaṭutti amaittalum, avaṟṟiṉ iyalpaiyum paṇpaiyum kaṇṭaṟitalumtāṉ kavitaiyiṉ toṭarnta uḻaippākum.”

ruciyak kaviñar sveṭṭēvāvum kavitait tōṟṟampaṟṟi, itē pōṉṟa karuttaiyē veḷiyiṭukiṟāḷ. “eṉ paṭaippukkaḷ ellāmē eṉ kēṭkum ceyal” eṉpatu avaḷ karuttu.

jermāṉiyak kaviñarrilkkiṉ aṉupavam ivarkaḷiṉiṉṟum caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭatu. ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭaiyil oru nāḷ taṉimaiyil amarntiruntapōtu, taṉ uḷḷattil nikaḻnta kavitai āvēcattai rilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu paṭarkkaiyil pativu ceykiṟār:

"iṉampuriyāta oṉṟu avaṉiṭam aṉṟu nikaḻntatu. vaḻakkampōl oru puttakattaik kaiyilēntiya vaṇṇam aṅkumiṅkumāka naṭantu koṇṭirunta avaṉ, kavaiyākap pirintirunta marakkiḷaiyoṉṟil vacatiyāka amarntu koṇṭāṉ. mikavum ōyvāka amarnta nilaiyil kaiyilirunta puttakattai mūṭi vaittu viṭṭuc cuṟṟiyirunta amaitiyāṉa, aḻakiya iyaṟkaic cūḻnilaiyil taṉṉai maṟantu mūḻkiṉāṉ.

"itaṟkumuṉ aṉupavittirāta ōr uṇarcci veḷḷam avaṉuḷ talaiyākap paravuvatai uṇarntāṉ. avaṉ amarntirunta marattiṉiṭaiyiliruntu avvuṇarcci melliya atirvukaḷāka avaṉuḷ iṟaṅkiyatu. itu pōṉṟa iṉpa atircciyai avaṉ eṉṟum aṉupavittatillai, avaṉ uṭampē āṉmāvākamāṟi iyalpāṉa taṉ ceyalkaḷai maṟantu ētō oru pērāṟṟalai taṉṉuḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu pōliruntatu; atuvumaṉṟi eḷitil pulappaṭātaiyaṟkaiyiṉ maṟupuṟattaik kaṇṭatu pōlavum iruntatu."

māyakōvski acāttiyat tuṇiccalkāraṉ. vāṉattil ēṟivarum pakalavaṉait tēnīr aruntak kūppiṭukiṟāṉ.

﻿ eṉṉa...?

kaṭavuḷē!

nāṉ vantirukkiṟēṉ.

kātil viḻukiṟatā?

uṉ toppiyaittukki

vaṇakkam cey!





eṉṟu vaḻippōkkaṉiṭam pēcuvatu pōl āṇṭavaṉiṭam tuṭukkākap pēcukiṟāṉ. karuttil maṭṭumaṉṟu; vaṭivattilum cila tuṇiccalāṉa muyaṟcikaḷai avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭāṉ.

kavitai ceṟivākavum, iṟukkamākavum irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai ellāk kaviñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ. jappāṉil haikkūvum, tamiḻil kuṟaḷum vaṭivattāl ciṟappup peṟṟavai. māyakōvski miṉ veṭṭukkaḷaip pōl paḷicciṭum tantik kavitai vaṭivam oṉṟait tōṟṟuvittirukkiṟāṉ.

tuppākki muḻaṅkaṭṭum

etiri ōṭa





kavitaiyai oru ‘varamākavum’ kalaitvētaiyiṉ kaṭaik kaṇ nōkkākavum ellārum pōṟṟuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl oru ruciya iḷaṅkaviñaṉ,

kavitaiyē!

eṉ turatirṣṭamē!

eṉ celvamē!

eṉ puṉitak kalaiyē!





eṉṟu pāṭukiṟāṉ. kavitaiyait ‘turatirṣṭam’ eṉṟu avaṉ kuṟippiṭak kāraṇam uṇṭu. kavitai ellārukkum pukaḻaiyum celvattaiyum vārik koṭuttu viṭuvatillai. nalla kavitai eḻutavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākac cilar taṅkaḷ vāḻkkai vacatiyaiyum, varuvāyaiyum, makiḻcciyaiyum, tiyākam ceyvatai iṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm. nalla kavitaikku avarkaḷ koṭukkum vilai ivai. eṉavē kavitai, kaviñaṉukku oruvakait turatirṣṭam tāṉē?

‘intiyam’ cila airōppiyak kaviñarkaḷāl matittup pōṟṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. eliyaṭ tamatu ciṟanta kāppiyamāṉa pāḻnilattai.

tattā, tayātavam, tamyatā

cānti cānti cānti





﻿eṉṟu upaniṭata varikaḷāl niṟaivu ceykiṟār. intiyac camayamāṉa peḷattattaiyum naṉku purintu koṇṭirukkiṟār. āṉāl teṉṉamerikkak kaviñaṉāṉa pāplō neruṭā,

aḻakiya nirvāṇap puttarkaḷ

matuviruntaip pārttavaṇṇam

tiṟanta veḷiyil

veṟumaiyākac

cirittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.





eṉṟu intiya nāṭṭaiyum, intiyac camayattaiyum kēli ceykiṟāṉ. innūlil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pattukkaviñarkaḷum mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ varalāṟē putukkavitai varalāṟu. taramāṉa kaviñarkaḷaiyum, taramāṉa kavitaikaḷaiyum purintu koṇṭu cuvaikka, innūl, niccayamāka utavum.

mēvār mīrāviṉ kuṟikkōḷ piruntāvaṉamum nantakumāraṉum; civakaṅkai mīrāviṉ kuṟikkōḷ nalla naṇparkaḷum nalla nūlkaḷum nāṉ avarukku nalla naṇpaṉ; nalla nūlum eḻutik koṭuttirukkiṟēṉ.

cēlam-16





aṉpuṭaṉ





1–1–93





muruku





﻿kalaic coṟkaḷ



aḻakiyam — Aestheticism

āṉmarakaciyam — Mysticism

iruppiyam — Existentialism

iruṇmai — Obscurity

kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitai — Vers libre

taṟkātaliyam — Narcissism

tuṇṭuk kavitai — Hack Verse

toṉmam — Myth

naṭappiyam — Functionalism

patippiyam — Impressionism

cevviyam — Classicism

puṟa meymmaiyiyam — Surrealism

puṟanilai oppīṭu — Objective co-relative

puṉaiviyam — Romanticism

poruḷ mutal vātam — Materialism

maṟuppiyam — Nihilism

mikaip puṉaiviyam — Romantic excess

meymmaiyiyam — Realism

veḷippāṭṭiyam — Expressionism.





paci, iṉpam, aṉpukkiṉiya tāy

ākiya ellāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka

nāṉ nēcippatu ilakkiyamē.





cārl pōtalēr

(1821-1867)



eṭkār ālaṉpō eṉṟa amerikka eḻuttāḷariṉ paṭaippiṉ mītu pōtalērukku aḷavu kaṭanta īṭupāṭu uṇṭu. ilakkiyat tuṟaiyil avarait tam kuruvākavum vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. atōṭu niṉṟiruntāl paravāyillai; avaruṭaiya paḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum ēṟṟuk koṇṭār.

ālaṉpō oru apiṉ piriyar: pōtalēr kañcāp piriyar. apiṉum kañcāvum payaṉpaṭuttupavarait tūkkattil āḻttuvatillai. avarkaḷ uḷḷattil oruvitak kiḷarcciyai ēṟpaṭutti, virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa kaṉavu nilaikku āṭpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttiyavuṭaṉ avarkaḷ uḷḷam entap pōkkil payaṇm ceykiṉṟatō, antap pōkkiṟku avarkaḷai entavitak kaṭṭuppāṭum illāmal celuttukiṉṟaṉa.

kañcā mayakkam ēṟpaṭuttum kuḻappamāṉa aṟputam paṟṟip pōtalēr piṉkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār: "kañcāp pōtaiyiṉ tuvakkattil, vaḻakkattiṟku māṟāṉa vaṇṇattiṉ ātikkam namakkup pulappaṭum. kaṇṇaip paṟṟikkum oḷiyuṭampōṭu kāṭci tarum mōkiṉip peṇkaḷ, nīla vāṉai viṭat teḷivāṉa āḻamāṉa viḻikaḷāl nammai uṟṟup pārppārkaḷ. appōtu ēṟpaṭum iṉam puriyāta kṣaṇanēra maṉanilaikaḷil nam vāḻkkaiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷum neḷivu cuḻivukaḷum, aṟputaṅkaḷum nam kaṇmuṉ paḷicciṭum. nam kaṇṇukku mutaṉ mutalil entap poruḷ teṉpaṭukiṟatō, atuvē nam aṟivai naṭattic cellum kuṟiyīṭāka amaiyum. teḷinta nīrnilaikaḷum, nilaik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum kaṉavulakattai namakkut tiṟantuviṭum; cōkamayamāṉa oru pēriṉpattil nam uḷḷam āḻntu, ellaiyaṟṟa kāṭcikaḷil tiḷaikkum."

ittakaiya aṉupavattil tiḷaittu mikavum nūtaṉamāṉa uṇarcci veḷippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya ariya kavitaikaḷaip paṭaitta kaviñar pōtalēr maṭṭum allar; vēṟupalarum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. makākavi pāratiyārukkum kañcāp paḻakkam uṇṭu; kuyilpāṭṭum inta aṭippaṭaiyil, avar virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa kaṉavu nilaik kaṟpaṉai tāṉ. kōlariṭjiṉ 'kupḷāykāṉ' eṉṟa kavitaiyaiyum, inta varicaiyil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

piyar cārl pōtalēr (Baudelaire Piere Charles) 1821 ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟa caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉariṉ makaṉākap pāricil piṟantār. ivar ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē tantai iṟantār tāy ōr irāṇuva atikāriyai maṟumaṇam ceytukoṇṭār. pōtalēr paḷḷik kalviyai muṭittukkoṇṭu caṭṭakkallūriyil nuḻaintār.

vīṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāta piḷḷaiyāka irunta ivaraik kappalēṟṟik kalkattāvukku aṉuppiṉar peṟṟōr. āṉāl naṭuvaḻiyil morīṣiyas tīvil iṟaṅkik koṇṭār. veppamaṇṭalattil irunta morīṣiyas tīvil cilakālam taṅkiyiruntu vetuvetuppāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷōṭu pārīs tirumpiṉār pōtalēr.

cutantaramāṉa paṇavacatiyōṭu irunta pōtalēr, pārīciṉ navanākarikak kēntiramāṉa 'lattīṉ kuvārṭṭars' eṉṟa pakutiyil kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa ullāca vāḻkkaiyil īṭupaṭṭār. 'ciṟappuk keṭṭavar' (decadent) eṉṟa peyarai virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. nīkrōk kalappu iṉak kārikai, karuppu rati ḻāṉ tuyvāl (Black Venus Jeanne Duval) eṉpavaḷ ivarukku nirantarak kātaliyākak kiṭaittāḷ. cīmāṭṭi caparttiyar eṉṟa naṭikaiyiṭamum avarukkut toṭarpiruntatu. pōtalēriṉ corkka narakak kaṟpaṉaikaḷ, ivviruvar mītum avar koṇṭa kātaṟ cuḻiyilē cuḻaṉṟaṭikkiṉṟaṉa.

iḷamaiyil avar virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa koḷkai 'ṭāṇṭiyicam'. taṉ viruppappaṭi vāḻtal, taṉmuṉaippōṭu mutaṉmaiyāka iruttal, naṭpu, nāṭu, kuṭumpam eṉṟa paṟṟukkaḷai aṟuttuc cutantā maṉitaṉāka vāḻtal, pirapukkaḷai veṟittal eṉpaṉa ṭāṇṭiyicattiṉ kuṟik kōḷkaḷ

“paci, iṉpam, aṉpukkiṉiya tāy ākiya ellāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka nāṉ nēcippatu ilakkiyamē” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum pōtalēr 'kalai kalaikkākavē' eṉṟu koḷkaiyuṭaiyavar. romāṇṭik kaviñarkaḷ virumpip pōṟṟum, 'poṅkavarum maṉaveḻuccik koḷkaiyai' ivar ēṟṟuk koḷvatillai. kalaikku oru teyvīka mariyātaiyum ivariṭattil kiṭaiyātu.

"aḻaku, aṟam, kalai yāvum ceyaṟkaiyāṉavai; avai kalaiñaṉ allatu kaviñaṉiṉ tiṭṭamiṭṭa cintaṉaiyiliruntu tōṉṟupavai. kavitaiyai entavitap piracārattukkum payaṉpaṭuttak kūṭātu. aḻakiṉ mītu aṭaṅkākkātal koḷḷutalum, uḷḷattiṉ āḻattiliruntu puṟappaṭum aḻaippukaḷukku aṭipaṇitalum tavirkka muṭiyātavai eṉṟa kāraṇattāl, kalaiñaṉum kaviñaṉum tām mēṟkoṇṭa paṭaippup paṇikku nirantara aṭimai" eṉṟa cintaṉaip pōkkuṭaiyavar pōtalēr.

kavitaiyiṉ karupporuḷ uyarntatākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttil pōtalērukku uṭaṉpāṭu kiṭaiyātu. aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil taṭṭuppaṭum cātāraṇap poruḷkaḷum, iḻinta poruḷkaḷumē aḻakāṉavai eṉṟum, apporuḷkaḷai aḻakiya collōviyattāl alaṅkarippatē mēlāṉa kavitai utti (Grand Style of Poetry) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār. ēḻai, kuṭikāraṉ, terup piccaikkāraṉ, vipacāri, apalai ākiyōrum paḻi, pāvam tīviṉai, pollāṅku ākiyavaiyum, avar kavitaikku virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa karupporuḷkaḷ. ikkoḷkaikku avar pāṭiyuḷḷa 'cūriyaṉ' eṉṟa kavitai ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭu, mēṟku vāyilil paṭiyum mālaic cūriyaṉiṉ varṇa jālattāl ulakiṉ cātāraṇap poruḷkaḷum eppaṭip pēraḻaku pūṇukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai appāṭṭil ciṟappittup pāṭukiṟār. ittakaiya racavātattai ēṟpaṭuttak kaviñaṉāṉavaṉ mappumantāramaṟṟa mēl vāṉattukkut taṉ oḷipaṭaitta kaṇkaḷai uyartta vēṇṭum; appōtu poruḷaṟṟa aṟpap poruḷkaḷum nuṭpamāṉa poruḷ viḷakkam peṟum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār pōtalēr. pōtalēriṉ ik koḷkaiyai mānakark koḷkai (The religious intoxication of great cities) eṉṟu tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.

‘the spantaneous cverflcw of powerful feelings’



mēlaināṭṭuk kalai ilakkiyap pōkkai aṭiyōṭu māṟṟiyamaitta āṟṟalmikka iyakkaṅkaḷāṉa aṭimaṉaviyam, kuṟiyīṭṭiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṟṟuk kaṇṇē pōtalērtāṉ eṉpatu ellōrum ēṟṟuk koṇṭa uṇmai. avaruṭaiya kavitai nūlāṉa 'naccup pūkkaḷ' (Les Fleurs du Mal) 1857 ām āṇṭu mutaṉmutalāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. atil teyva nintaṉai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟic camayavātikaḷ kaṇṭaṉak kuāl eḻuppiṉar. paṭippavar uḷḷattil kīḻttaramāṉa uṇarccikaḷai avar kavitaikaḷ tūṇṭuvatākak kuṟṟam cāṭṭi pireñcu aracāṅkam 300 pirāṅk avarukku aparātam vitittatu.

avarukkup piṉ vanta cantatiyiṉariṭaiyē naccup pūkkaḷaip pōl aparimitamāṉa celvākkuppeṟṟa vēṟu kavitai nūl mēlai nāṭṭil etuvumillai eṉṟu collalām. mēlai nāṭṭiṉ kavitaip putumaiyē (Modernism in Poetry) in nūlil toṭaṅkukiṟatu.

putuk kaviñarkaḷiṉ muṉmātiriyē (Archtype) pōtalērtāṉ. putuk kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷāṉa varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa taṉimai, etirppuṇarcci, maṉac cañcalam, taṉṉaiyē aḻittuk koḷḷum āttira uṇarcci yāvum pōtalēriṭamiruntu peṟṟavaiyē. “piṟar tūṟṟumpaṭi puraṭcikaramāka vāḻnta pōtalēruṭaiya vāḻkkaiyiṉ aṭikkuṟippup paṟṟiya varalāṟē, putuk kavitaiyiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci paṟṟiya varalāṟākum” eṉṟu mēlai nāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷar kuṟippiṭuvatu poruttamē.

“kaṟpaṉai eṉpatu aṟiviyal aṭippaṭaiyil amainta pērāṟṟal. pēraṇṭattiṉ oḻuṅkaiyum iyakkattaiyum kaṟpaṉai āṟṟalāl tāṉuṇara muṭiyum” eṉṟu pōtalēr kuṟippiṭṭār. svīṭaṉparkkum ālaṉpōvum itē koḷkaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ.

pōtalēr ōr aiyuṟavāḷar (Sceptic). kaṟpaṉai oru pērāṟṟal eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭālum atu kai koṭuttu utavāta cila nēraṅkaḷil ataṉ mītu avarukku aiyaṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. anta nēraṅkaḷil kaṟpaṉaikku ūkkam koṭuppataṟkāka matuvaiyum, kañcāvaiyum nāṭiṉār. avaṟṟiṉ tūṇṭutalāl oru ceyaṟkaic corkkattai avarē paṭaittukkoṇṭu atil tiḷaittār.

pōtalēr kaṭavuṭpaṟṟō matappaṟṟō illātavar. eṉṟālum avar uḷḷattil paṭintuviṭṭa camayakkaruttukkaḷ cilavaṟṟai avarāl utaṟa muṭiyavillai. kiṟittuva camayak koḷkaippaṭi, maṉitaṉ pāviyākavē irukkiṟāṉ eṉṟa uṇarvum, ātāmum ēvāḷum pāvam ceyvataṟkumuṉ vāḻnta 'ētēṉ' eṉṟ corkkattai maṉitaṉ mīṇṭum aṭaiyamuṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇarvum, ivvulakiṉ tuṉpamākiya kōrappiṭiyiliruntu yārum viṭupaṭamuṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇarvum pōtalērai āṭṭippaṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. ittuṉpa ulakiṉ piṭiyiliruntu viṭupaṭṭuttaṉ āṉmāvaip paravacappaṭuttuvataṟkāka, avarē paṭaittuk koṇṭatutāṉ ceyaṟakaic corkkam!'

pōtalēriṉ naccuppūkkaḷmūṉṟukottāka malarntirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutalkottu avar uḷaccōrvaiyum, iraṇṭāvatu kottu pāris nakarak kāṭcikaḷaiyum, mūṉṟāvatu kottu ceyaṟkaic corkkattai nōkkiya avar payaṇattaiyum, vivarikkiṉṟaṉa. iḷamaiyil kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟi vāḻnta tam iḻitta vāḻkkaiyaip palapāṭalkaḷil avar tirumpip pārttu uḷaccōrvu koḷkiṟār. tammai vāṭṭum uḷac cōrvil aḻuntippōvatā allatu ceyaṟkaic corkkattil paṟappatā eṉṟa ūcalāṭṭam pala pāṭalkaḷil kaṟpaṉai nayattōṭu eṭutturaikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. pala pāṭalkaḷil ceyaṟkaic corkkattil avar aṭaiyum paravacanilai pēcappaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇṇāl peṟum aimpulaṉ iṉpattaik kaṟpaṉai ceyyāmal oli, oḷi, maṇam ivaṟṟāl peṟum iṉpaṅkaḷaiyē kaṟpaṉaiceykiṟār pōtalēr. avaruṭaiya corkkattil varum aṭimaikaḷum ēvalarkaḷum nirvāṇamākak kāṭcitarukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷuṭaiya nirvāṇam, ivvulaka nākarikattiṉ tīkkaraṅkaḷāl tīṇṭappaṭāta tūymaiyiṉ uruvakam.

ceyaṟkaic corkkattil pōtalēr aṭikkaṭi tiḷaittup paravacappaṭṭālum, cāvaip paṟṟiya eṇṇam avar uḷḷattai viṭṭu nīṅkavillai. naccuppūkkaḷiṉ iṟutiyil uḷḷa 'payaṇam' eṉṟa pāṭṭil tām kaṭaliṉ alai vayiṟṟil āḻntuviṭa viruppam terivikkiṟār. tām ceṉṟaṭaiyavirumpum kuṟikkōḷ corkkamākairuntālum cari, narakamāka iruntālum cari, iṉan teriyāta oṉṟaṉ āḻattil tām mūḻkiviṭa virumpukiṟār. 'ēḻaiyiṉ cāvu' eṉaṟa pāṭalil cāvai makiḻcci tarum iṉpa uṇarvōṭu varavēṟṟup pāṭukiṟār.

cāvē!

teyvīkap perumpukaḻē!

tēṭalukkup pulappaṭāta

kaṟpaṉaik kaḷañciyamē!

ēḻai virumpum pōtu

eṭuttuc celviṭum

paramparaic cottē!

purintu koḷḷa muṭiyāta

vāṉulakiṉ vāyiṟ katavē!





eṉṟu cāvait taṉ caṅkīta varikaḷāl alaṅkarikki ceyaṟkaic corkkattup paravaca nilaiyaikkūṭa maraṇat tuṉpattaik kalantutāṉ aṉupavittār. [1]'citiriyāp payaṇam' eṉṟa kavitaiyil, kātal tīvē avarukkuk kaṟai paṭintu kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.

vāṉam—

teḷinta aḻakōṭu

kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.

kaṭalum—

āḻnta amaitiyuṭaṉ

paṭuttirukkiṟatu.

āṉāl—

eṅkum iruḷ:

tiṭṭut tiṭṭāṉa

rattak kaṟaikaḷ

eṉ itayam—

oru muraṭṭuk kākitattil

curuṭṭap paṭṭup

putaintu kiṭappatāka

eṉakkuḷ ōr uruvakam.



ō viṉasē!

uṉ kātal tīvil

oru kaṟpaṉait

tūkku marattil

eṉa uruvam

toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu.

āṇṭavaṉē!

eṉ uṭampaiyum

itayattaiyum

veṟuppillāmal pārkka

eṉakku āṟṟalaik koṭu!





eṉṟu vēṇṭukiṟār pōtalēr.

avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭaṟpuṟavai (Albatross) eṉṟa pāṭal avaruṭaiya avastaiyiṉ kuṟiyīṭākavum, naccup pūkkaḷiṉ maiyak karuttākavum amaintuḷḷatu. ālpaṭrās eṉṟa kaṭaṟpaṟavai nīṇṭa iṟakkaikaḷai uṭaiyatu, atai vāṉamaṇṭalattaracaṉ (The prince of the clouds) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. vāṉattai aṉāyācamāka aḷakka utavum apperiya ciṟakukaḷē. pūmiyai aṭaintatum ataṟkupperiya cumaiyāka māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟait tūkkikkoṇṭu appaṟavaiyāl aṭiyeṭuttu naṭakkamuṭivatillai.

pōtalēr tamatu āṟṟal mikka kaṟpaṉaiyiṉ utaviyōṭu corkkamaṇṭalattil paṟantālum, ivvulakap paṟṟum, ciṟṟiṉpa iccaiyum tammaip piṇittu muṭakki viṭuvatāka eṇṇi varuntukiṟār; tām āḻntirukkum tuṉpak kaṭaliliruntu mīṭciyē kiṭaiyātu eṉṟa varuttam aṭikkaṭi avariṭam mēlōṅki niṟpatuṇṭu.

pāris nakaram

māṟik koṇṭirukkiṟatu

eṉṉai aḻuttum

tuyarac cumai

ciṟitu kūṭa acaiyavillai





eṉṟu avaruṭaiya tuyarap perumūccu orupāṭalil kēṭkiṟatu. maṟṟoru pāṭalil,

eṉ tuyaramē

aṟivōṭu naṭantukoḷ;

amaitiyāka iru!

iraviṉ koṭumaikkañci

nī kataṟukiṟāy?

iravu viḻuntuviṭṭatu

itō—

viṭiyal talai tūkkukiṟatu:





eṉṟu ñāṉipōl tamakkuttāmē āṟutal kūṟik koḷkiṟār.

eṉṟālum avar vāḻvil miñciyatu ēmāṟṟamē. avar eḻutiyuḷḷa uraip pāṭalkaḷil, vāḻviṉ iṟutiyil avar koṇṭa calippum, ēmāṟṟamum urukkamākap pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

"cila camayaṅkaḷil eṉṉai oru ñāṉiyeṉṟu nāṉ muṭṭāḷtaṉamāka niṉaittuk koḷvatuṇṭu. oru maruttuvaṉukku iruppatupōl, aruḷuḷḷam eṉṉiṭattil amaiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāṉ avāvuvatuṇṭu; appēṟu eṉakkuk kiṭaikkappōvatillai. ivvañcaka ulakil eṉṉai nāṉē tolaittuviṭṭu, makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉ puṟaṅkaiyāl iṭittut taḷḷappaṭṭu alaintu tirintu koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. eṉ kaṭantakāla niṉaivukaḷiṉ āḻattai aṭikkaṭi eṭṭip pārttu nāṉ mikavum kaḷaittup pōyviṭṭēṉ. ēmāṟṟamum, kacappuṇarcciyum, tuṉpamā-mīṭciyā eṉṟu uṇara muṭiyāta kuḻappamumē eṉakku miñciyavai eṉṟu pulampukiṟār pōtalēr.

mēkattiṉ ucciyil paṟappataṟku utaviya periya ciṟakukaḷiṉ cumai, pūmiyil kaṭaṟpaṟavaiyaic ceyalaṟṟatākkuvatupōl, kaṟpaṉaic corkkattil paṟappataṟku ivarukku utaviyāka irunta ivaratu ciṟakukaḷē (kañcā, aḷavaṟṟa matuppaḻakkam) kaṭaici nāṭkaḷil ivarai muṭakkip pōṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. pakkavātam, ivarai muṭakkippōṭṭup paṭukkaiyil taḷḷiviṭṭatu. 'cipilis' ivaraic citaittu viṭṭatu.

nīṇṭanāḷ kañcāp paḻakkattiṉ kaṭaici mailkal, talai cuṟṟalum (Vertigo) paittiyamumtāṉ. taṉ kaṭaici nilaiyaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu "iṉpamum atircciyum koṇṭa iḻuppu nōy (Hysteria) eṉakkup paḻakkamākip pōyviṭṭatu. toṭarnatu talaicuṟṟalāl nāṉ avatippaṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. 23-1-1862 ām nāḷākiya iṉṟu, etirpārāta ōr eccarikkai eṉakkuk kiṭṭiyatu. paṟantu cellum paittiyac ciṟaku vīcciṉ kāṟṟu, eṉmītu paṭintu ceṉṟatai nāṉ uṇarntēṉ" eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

taṉpeyaraikkūṭa niṉaivukūra muṭiyāta nilaiyilum, nilaik kaṇṇāṭiyil taṉ conta mukattaiyē aṭaiyāḷam kaṇṭukoḷḷa muṭiyāta nilaiyilum, puṟakkaṇippukku āḷāki, ātaravaṟṟut tamatu kaṭaici nāṭkaḷaip pāricil kaḻittār. mikac ciṟanta kaviñarāṉa pōtalēraip pukaḻpeṟṟa pireñcu ilakkiyak kaḻakam (French Academy) uṟuppiṉarāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa maṟantuviṭṭatu. anta vētaṉaiyait tāḷāta avar, pāricai viṭṭu nīṅki peljiyattil coṟpoḻivup payaṇam oṉṟai mēṟkoṇṭār. alaiccal avar uṭalai mikavum pātittatu. 1867il pārīs maruttuvamaṉaiyoṉṟil uyirtuṟantār.

tām ceyyum entap paṇiyaiyum, ceppamākavum, tiruttamākavum ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkaiyuṭaiyavar pōtalēr. niṟaiya eḻutuvatai avar virumpavillai. oṉṟu ceytālum, oppaṟṟatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu avar viruppam. naccuppūkkaḷai 1850-il toṭaṅkiṉār. ataṉ kuṟaikaḷaik kaḷaintu, pattāṇṭukaḷ tiruttam ceytu, mēṉmēlum aḻakupaṭuttiṉār. 1861-il ataṉ iṟuti vaṭivam uruppeṟṟatu.

pōtalēriṉ paṭaippukaḷ paṟṟi ārtar caimaṉs eṉṟa tiṟaṉāyvāḷar, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa "ilakkiyattil kuṟiyīṭṭu iyakkam' (The Symbolist Movement in Literature) eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār:

"orē oru kavitai nūlai eḻutuvatil pōtalēr tamatu vāḻkkai muḻuvataiyum celaviṭṭār; ataṉ piṟakutāṉ pireñcu moḻiyil uyarnta kavitaikaḷ ellām tōṟṟam eṭuttaṉa.



"ōr urai naṭai nūl eḻutiṉār; anta urainaṭai aḻakuk kalaiyāka (Fine Art) matikkappaṭukiṟatu.

oru tiṟaṉāyvu nūl eḻutiṉār. avar vāḻnta kālattil tōṉṟiya tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil ituvē uyarntatum uṇmaiyāṉatum nuṭpamāṉatum ākum.

orumoḻipeyarppuc ceytār; atu mūlattai viṭac ciṟappāṉatu”.

pōtalēr eḻutiyuḷḷa uraippākkaḷ (pāriciṉ iḻi nilai’[2]) aimpatu irukkum. avai etukaiyō, icaiyoḻuṅkō illāmal eḻutappaṭṭavai ; uḷḷatatiṉ uṇarccikaḷukkēṟṟa ōcaiyoḻuṅkum taṉṉai maṟanta cintaṉaiyiṉ alaivīccum koṇṭavai; yārum eḷitil piṉpaṟṟa muṭiyātapaṭi kalainuṇukkamum, vēlaippāṭum mikkavai; uraippākkaḷ varalāṟṟil mutaṉmaiyākak kuṟippiṭattakkavai; cila paṇpukaḷil oppaṟṟavai; ilakkiyaccōtaṉai muyaṟciyil pārāṭṭattakka ariya veṟṟi.

tilākriks eṉṟa ōviyar paṟṟiyum, tāmiyar, māṉeṭ, ḵpḷāparṭ, vēkṉar eṉṟa eḻuttāḷarkaḷ paṟṟiyum avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭṭuraikaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvukkalai vaḷarccipeṟāta kālattil eḻutappaṭṭavai. akkaṭṭuraikaḷaipa peruma ilakkiya cātaṉaiyākap piṟkāla aṟiñarkaḷ pārāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.

amerikka eḻuttāḷar ālaṉpōviṉ kataikaḷaip pōtalēr pireñcu moḻiyil moḻipeyarttirukkiṟār. mūlattiṉ ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmalum, mūlattaiviṭac ciṟanta kalaiyamcattuṭaṉum atu ceyyappaṭṭiruppatāka ellārum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. amerikka makkaḷiṭaiyē inta moḻi peyarppukku nalla varavēṟpu uḷḷatu.

1865 ām āṇṭil tamatu paṭaippukkaḷ paṟṟit tam tāyiṭam kuṟippiṭum pōtu “eṉ paṭaippiṉ aḷavu ciṟiyatāka irukkalām; āṉāl acciṟiya paṭaippu eṉ tuyaram mikka kaṭiṉa uḻaippiṉ veḷippāṭu” eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟār

1920 ām āṇṭil āntirēkaiṭ eṉṟa aṟiñar pōtalēraip paṟṟip pattirikaiyil kuṟippiṭumpōtu “avaruṭaiya ovvoru muyaṟciyum, avaratu āṉmāviṉ paritavippākavum, oppaṟṟa pōrāṭṭamākavum amaintuḷḷatu” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. paṭittavar uḷḷattil eṉṟum nilaittu niṟkumpaṭi eṭkār ālaṉ pōvaip paṟṟip pōtalēr uṇarcci pūrvamāka oru karuttai veḷiyiṭṭār. “iṉṟu nam makiḻccikku ūṟṟāka iruppatu (avar paṭaippu) etuvō, atuvē avaraik koṉṟuviṭṭatu” eṉṟār. antak karuttu pōtalērukkum poruntum.





↑ citiriyā-kirēkkak kātal tēvatai vīṉas vīṟṟirukkum tīvu.

↑ Spleen de Paris.





* * *





eṉṉāl kātalaic

cāḷarattukku veḷiyē

tūkki eṟiya muṭiyātu.





ārtar rempō

(1854–1891)



rempō pireñcu nāṭṭu ñāṉacampantaṉ piñcupparuvattil eḻutat toṭaṅkit taṉatu 19 ām vayatil-kavitait toḻilai muṭittuk koṇṭavaṉ. aintāṇṭuk kālattil avaṉ eḻutiya coṟpak kavitaikaḷ airōppiyak kavitaip pōkkaiyē māṟṟiyamaikkum āṟṟal peṟṟavaiyāka viḷaṅkiṉa. avaṉ kavitai vāḻkkai kaṇa nērattil miṉṉalaip pōṉṟatu; cīṟum puyalaip pōṉṟatu. kōṭai maḻaiyaip pōl tiṭīreṉṟu koṭṭi viṭṭu ōyntu pōṉavaṉ rempō inta vāmaṉa vacantattaip pārāṭṭāta mēlai nāṭṭu ilakkiyavāti yārumillai.

pireñcu nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil cārlivil eṉṟa ciṟiya nakaril 1954-il piṟantāṉ rempō. ivaṉ tantai ḵpreṭrik rempō pireñcup paṭaiyil ‘kēpṭaṉ’ patavi vakittavar. avar irāṇuvap paṇi perumpālum aljīriyāvil kaḻintatu. ḵpreṭrik rempō arapu moḻiyai naṉku kaṟṟavar. kurāṉai pireñcil moḻi peyarttavar.

kaviñaṉ rempōviṉ tāy, kaṇṭippum aṭaṅkāp piṭārit taṉamum mikkavaḷ. avaḷōṭu vāḻat tākkup piṭikka muṭiyāmal, kaviñaṉ rempō āṟuvayatuc ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē, avaṉ tantai ḵpreṭrik vīṭṭai viṭṭu ōṭi viṭṭār. ataṉ piṟaku rempōvō, avaṉ tāyō avarai mīṇṭum cantikkavē illai.

rempō pattu vayatup paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē ati nuṭpamum, piṭivātamum, putumai viruppamum pōkkirittaṉamum uṭaiyavaṉākak kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil mutaṉ mutalāka avaṉ eḻutiya ilattīṉ kavitai avaṉukkup paḷḷip paricaip peṟṟuk koṭuttatu. atu iṭaiyar vāḻkkaiyaik kūṟum nāṭṭup puṟappāṭal[1], patiṉāṉkām vayatil avaṉ eḻutiya ‘ēḻuvayatukkaviñaṉ’ (Seven-year-old poet) eṉṟa kavitaiyil taṉ aṟivu nuṭpattait tāṉē pārāṭṭi eḻutiyirukkiṟāṉ. etir kālattil avaṉ nikaḻttavirukkum piramippūṭṭum cātaṉaikaḷukku ik kavitaikaḷ kaṭṭiyaṅ kūṟuvatōṭu, avaṉ oru navīṉa ārḵpis[2] eṉpataiyum nilai nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

iyaṟkaiyilēyē viṭutalai vēṭkaiyum piṭivātamum koṇṭa rempōvukkut tāyiṉ kaṇṭippum aṭakku muṟaiyum aṭiyōṭu piṭikkavillai, paḻamaiyil ūṟip pōṉa naṭuttara varkkattiṉ vāḻkkaiyil avaṉukku veṟuppum calippum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aṭikkaṭi tāyiṭam collāmal ūrai viṭṭu ōṭuvatum, tirumpuvatumāka iruntāṉ. intac camayattil tāṉ kavitai veṟi avaṉaip paṟṟik koṇṭatu.

1871 ām āṇṭu taṉ patiṉēḻām vayatil pāricukku ōṭiya pōtu, aṅkirunta pukaḻpeṟṟa kaviñarākiya pālverlēṉaic cantikkum vāyppu avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. rempō verlēṉiṉ kuṭumpattil oruvaṉākavē iṭam peṟṟāṉ. āṉāl verlēṉiṉ tāyum maṉaiviyum rempōvai veṟuttaṉar. avaṉuṭaiya vicittiramāṉa pēccum, koḷkaiyum, oḻuṅkīṉamāṉa naṭai muṟaikaḷum, paṭukkaiyil iruntapaṭiyē pukaipiṭikkum paḻakkamum avarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭōṭu piṭikkavillai. itaṉāl verlēṉiṉ tampatiyāriṭaiyē maṇamuṟivu ēṟpaṭṭatu. rempōvukkum verlēṉukkum iṭaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa takāta pāluṟavup paḻakkam, kaṭaiciyil tuppākkic caṇṭaiyil muṭintatu. 1974 ām āṇṭil ivarkaḷ iṭaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa inta uṟavu muṟivu rempōviṉ kavitai vāḻkkaikku aṭiyōṭu muṟṟuppuḷḷi vaittuviṭṭatu. verlēṉuṭaiya kavitaip paṇiyilum carivu ēṟpaṭṭatu.

taṉatu pattoṉpatām vayatil kavitai eḻutuvatai viṭṭuviṭṭu, airōppāvilum mattiyak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷilum, āppirikkāvilum cuṟṟiyalaintāṉ. appōtu piḻaippukkāka avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa paṇikaḷ pala. koñcanāḷ icaippayiṟciyum, piṟamoḻip payiṟciyum mēṟkoṇṭāṉ; ṭaccuk kālaṉip paṭaiyil cērntu cilakālam paṇi purintāṉ. caipracil kaṭṭiṭa mēstiriyāka vēlai pārttāṉ. kaṭaiciyil apicīṉiyāvil ‘arārē’ eṉṟa iṭattil nilaiyākat taṅki kāḵpi, kaḷḷattuppākki, yāṉait tantam mutaliyavaṟṟai viṟpaṉai ceytatōṭu, nīkrō aṭimaikaḷaiyum pireñcu vaṇikarkaḷukku viṟpaṉai ceytāṉ. iṅku vāḻnta camayattil ivaṉ tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

1891-ām āṇṭil muḻaṅkālil puṟṟunōy ēṟpaṭṭu, ataṟku maruttuvam ceytu koḷvataṟkāka pirāṉcu tirumpiṉāṉ. mārcēls maruttuvamaṉaiyoṉṟil kāl tuṇṭikkappaṭṭu iṟantāṉ. appōtu-avaṉukku vayatu muppattēḻu. taṅkaḷiṭaiyē uṟavu muṟinta nilaiyilum, verlēṉ rempōviṉuṭaiya kavitaikaḷait tiraṭṭi oḻuṅku ceytār. rempō iṟappataṟku ōrāṇṭukkumuṉ, verlēṉ arārēvukkuk kaṭitam eḻuti, ētāvatu putiya kavitaikaḷ eḻutiyiruntāl aṉuppivaikkumpaṭi rempōvaik kēṭṭuk koṇṭār. ataṟku rempō, antak kuppaiyai nāṉ tīṇṭuvatu kūṭa illai’ eṉṟu patil eḻutiyiruntāṉ. rempō iṟantapiṟakē avaṉ kavitaikaḷ muṟaiyākat tokuttu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ vāḻkaiyaip paṟṟiyum, kavitaip paṇipaṟṟiyum pala kaṟpaṉaikaḷum kaṭṭuk kataikaḷum paravalāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

oru muṟai pireñcu nāṭṭiṉ peruṅ kaviñarum nāvalāciriyarumāṉa viktar hyūkō rempōvaik ‘kuḻantai ṣēkspiyar’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭatāka avaṉiṭam kūṟiya poḻutu ‘eṉakku iṇaiyāka yāraiyum oppiṭṭuc colla muṭiyātu’ eṉṟu perumitamākac coṉṉāṉām.

rempō taṉatu iṟappukkup piṟaku, taṉatu kavitaikaḷōṭu niṟaiyak kaṭitaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟippukkaḷaiyum viṭṭuc ceṉṟirukkiṟāṉ. eṉṟālum mēlai nāṭṭu ilakkiyavātikaḷukku ivaṉ periya putirākavē viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. rempōviṉ cārlivil nakaril, avvūr makkaḷ avaṉukkuc cilaiyeṭuttuc ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar.

rempōvukkuc caṟṟu muṉpu vāḻnta kaviñar pōtalēr, ivaṉukku vāḻkkait tuṟaiyilum, ilakkiyat tuṟaiyilum muṉṉōṭi eṉṟu collalām. vāḻkkaiyaik kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟi viruppampōl cuvaittuc ‘ciṟappuk keṭṭavar’ eṉṟa peyarait tāmē ēṟṟuk koṇṭa pireñcu aruṇakiri pōtalēr. rempō avarai appaṭiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉār. tāyiṉ irakkamaṟṟa aṭakkumuṟaiyum, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa iḷamaiyum, piṭivātamum pōkkirittaṉamum pāluṟavu vakkiramum rempōviṉ paṭaippukkaḷil piratipalikkak kāṇalām.

pōtalēriṉ mānakarak koḷkaiyaiyum rempō appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. taṉatu naṇpar verlēṉuṭaṉ ilaṇṭaṉ, pirassals, pāricu nakara vītikaḷil cuṟṟittirintāṉ. cākkaṭai yōrattilum, matuppuraikaḷilum vipaccāra viṭutikaḷilum iravaik kaḻittāṉ. mānakaraṅkaḷiṉ iruṭṭu vāḻkkai avaṉukkup paḻakippōṉa oṉṟu. nīlavāṉilum, kuḷir cōlaiyilum, aruviyilum, cilirkkum teṉṟalilum, cirikkum pūkkaḷilum potuvākak kaviñarkaḷ uḷḷattaip paṟikoṭuttuk kavitaikaḷ eḻutuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl maṉita vāḻviṉ uyirttuṭippai nakara vāḻkkaiyiṉ avalaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil rempō kaṇṭāṉ. nakariṉ aḻukal avaṉukkuk kavitai nākarikamākiviṭṭatu. nakaraṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa naṭaimuṟai, amaitiyiṉmai, cuṟucuṟuppāṉa iravu vāḻkkai, cērikaḷiṉ iḻinta nilai, toḻiṟ cālaikaḷiṉ ōcai, pukai paṭinta cuṟṟuc cūḻal yāvum avaṉ kavitaiyiṉ karupporuḷkaḷāka iṭam peṟṟaṉa.

piḷḷaip paruvattilum viṭalaip paruvattilum tāyiṉ aṭakku muṟaikku āṭpaṭṭu varuntiya pōtu avaṉaiyum aṟiyāmal avaṉ aṭimaṉattil peṇkaḷiṉ mītu oru veṟuppuṇarcci paṭintiruntatu. mutaṉ mutalil vīṭṭai viṭṭu ōṭiyapōtu peṇmaiyaip paṟṟi oru viyappārvamum, iṉampuriyāta kuṟukuṟuppum avaṉuḷḷattil kuṭi koṇṭiruntaṉa. peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattil nuḻaiyum pōtu puṟaccamaya ulakattil tēvataikaḷukku oppāka nuḻaiyum vīraṉākat taṉṉaik kaṟpaṉai ceytu koṇṭāṉ. āṉāl appōtu avaṉ ārvattōṭu cantitta peṇkaḷ muraṇṭu piṭippavarkaḷākavē avaṉukku vāyttaṉar. avarkaḷ cantippāl ēṟpaṭṭa aṉupavaṅkaḷaic cūriyaṉum tacaiyum (Sun and Flesh) kēli (The Tease) paccai naṭaṉac cālaiyil (At the Green Cabaret) alaiccal (Wandering) niṉā eṉṉa patiliṟuttāḷ (What Nina Answered) ākiya kavitaikaḷil pativu ceykiṟāṉ.

iraṇṭāvatu muṟai vīṭṭai viṭṭup pāricukku ōṭiyapōtu pireñcup puraṭci muṭintu kamyūṉkaḷiṉ āṭci aṅkē naṭaipeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntatu. avvāṭciyāl pāricil ēṟpaṭṭa kuḻappamum, kolaiyum, paṭṭiṉiyum rempōviṉ uḷḷattil oru vaṟaṭciyaiyum nampikkaiyiṉmaiyaiyum tōṟṟuvittaṉa. makkaḷāṭciyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟum enta amaippaiyum avaṉ veṟuttāṉ. taṉimaiyum avanampikkaiyum avaṉuḷḷattil oru vakai arājakattattuvattait tōṟṟuvittaṉa. pāricu nakarameṅkum cāvaiyum kaṇṇīraiyumē cantittāṉ. vaḻiyil iṟantu kiṭanta oru paṭaivīraṉiṉ piṇattaip pārttu uḷḷam kacintatu. camaveḷiyil tūṅkupavaṉ (The Sleeper and The Valley) eṉṟa pāṭalai eḻutiṉāṉ.

1871-il mūṉṟām muṟai vīṭṭai viṭṭu ōṭiyapōtu mikaiyāṉa kātaṟ kaḷiyāṭṭattil mūḻkiyatāl peṇmaiyiṉ mītu calippum viraktiyum avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. kāttiruppavarkaḷ (Sitters) eṉ ciṟiya kātalikaḷ (My Little Loveiies) aṉat yō meṉ rati (Wenus Anadyomene) eṉṟa kavitaikaḷil peṇkaḷ mītu koṇṭa veṟuppaip pativu ceyvatōṭu, avarkaḷ pōlittaṉattaiyum, cākacattaiyum tōlurittuk kāṭṭukiṟāṉ. kātalukkup putup poruḷ kaṇṭaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ. appōtutāṉ verlēṉiṉ putiya uṟavu avaṉukku ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

mutiriḷamaip paruvattil ēṟpaṭak kūṭiya muḻumai peṟāta pāluṇarvu viruppaṅkaḷum accaṅkaḷum mutaṉmutalāka rempōviṉ kavitaikaḷil mika nuṇukkamāka ilakkiya vaṭivam peṟṟaṉa. oru pāṭal:

avaṉ

taṉatu ēḻāvatu vayatil

pālaikaḷilum

vaṉāntaraṅkaḷilum

iḷamarak kāṭukaḷilum

kaṭaṟkaraikaḷilum

cutantaramākac cuṟṟittiriyum

uyirkaḷaippaṟṟiya

iṉpakkaṟpaṉaikaḷil īṭupaṭṭuk

kavitaikaḷ vaṭittāṉ.



vaṇṇappaṭa vāra itaḻkaḷ

avaṉ kaṟpaṉaiyait

tuṇṭiviṭṭaṉa.

avaṟṟuḷ veḷiyākiyirunta

speyiṉ ittāliyap

peṇkaḷiṉ aṇivakuppu

avaṉai-

nāṇap paṭuttiyatu.





cīṭṭittuṇi āṭaiyaṇintu

aṭuttavīṭṭu

eṭṭu vayatu muraṭṭucciṟumi

ōṭivantu-

avaṉ mītu tāvi viḻuntāḷ

avaṉai-

ōraṅkaṭṭiṉāḷ,

avaṉmītu

cavāri ceytāḷ,

toṅkum kūntalai

acaittuc cirittāḷ,



avaḷaṭiyil

akappaṭṭuk koṇṭaavaṉ





[ēḻuvayatuk kaviñaṉ]

‘ciṟappuk keṭṭavar’ eṉṟu peyareṭuttālum kiṟittavarāṉa pōtalēr, taṉ camyattaiyō, āṉmāvaiyō, muktip peruvāḻvaiyō tiyākamceyyat tayārāka illai, āṉāl, ‘rempō taṉimaṉita āḷumai, āṉmā, muktiyāvum kālattiṟ kovvāta taṟperumaikaḷ’ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ. oru piṭivātamāṉa uṇarvōṭum, tuṟavu maṉappāṉmaiyōṭum leḷakīka āṉmīkap perumaikaḷai avaṉ veṟuttotukkiṉāṉ. kaṭaici mūccu irukkum varai maṉita nērmaikku matippuk koṭukkāta, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa vāḻkkaiyē vāḻntāṉ.

eṉṟālum, ‘ēmāṟṟappaṭṭa itayam’ (The Cheated Heart) eṉṟa kavitaiyil ‘eṉatu mīṭcikku eṉṉa vaḻi?’ eṉṟa pāviyiṉ viṉāvai avaṉē eḻuppik koḷkiṟāṉ. kiṟittava camayattai ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta avaṉukkuc camayattīrvu eppaṭik kiṭaikkum? eṉavē avaṉ, tāṉē taṉṉaiyoru ñāṉiyākkik koṇṭu, taṉatu pāva vimōcaṉattiṟkāṉa vaḻiyai 15-5-1871-il taṉatu naṇpaṉ pālṭemṉikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ:

“oru kaviñaṉāka māṟa virumpum maṉitaṉ taṉṉai muḻumaiyāka āyntu aṟikiṟāṉ; taṉ āṉmāvait tēṭi ataik kaṇkāṇikkiṟāṉ; piṉṉar cōtittup pārkkiṟāṉ; kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟāṉ. āṉmāvai uṇarntu koṇṭapiṉ ataip paṇpaṭuttukiṟāṉ”

itutāṉ rempō taṉ mīṭcikkum muktikkum vakuttuk koṇṭatiṭṭam, eṉṟālum pirassals maruttuvamaṉaiyil cāvukkākap pōrāṭik koṇṭiruntapōtu kattōlikka camayattai ēṟṟuk koṇṭu pāva maṉṉippu peṟukiṟāṉ.

rempōviṉ kavitaikaḷ ‘narakattil oru paruvam, [3](A Season In Hell) veḷicaṅkaḷ (Illuminations) eṉṟa iraṇṭu tokutikaḷāka veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa. narakattil oru paruvattait ‘taṉ paṭaippukkaḷiṉ veṟṟi’ eṉṟu taṉ naṇpaṉ pāl ṭerṉikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil avaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. 1873 ām āṇṭuk kōṭaiyil innūl eḻutappaṭṭu, 1875 ām āṇṭil avaṉuṭaiya contac celavil patippikkappaṭṭatu. innūlai accil kāṇa avaṉ mikavum virumpiṉāṉ. kavitait toḻilaik kaiviṭṭuviṭṭup piḻaippukkāka mattiyak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷil avaṉ cuṟṟit tirinta pōtu, avaṉuṭaiya naṇparkaḷum pāricu nakara ilakkiyavātikaḷum kūṭi 1868-il ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟa avaṉatu iraṇṭām kavitait tokutiyai veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. appōtu rempōviṉ naṭamāṭṭattaip paṟṟi aṟiyāta kāraṇattāl avaṉ iṟantuviṭṭatākavē ellārum niṉaittaṉar.

rempōviṉ iraṇṭu kavitait tokutikaḷil etu mutalil eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟa carccai pireñcu ilakkiya vātikaḷiṭaiyē nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷāka iruntu koṇṭirukkiṟatu. veḷiccaṅkaḷil cila kavitaikaḷai eḻutiya piṟaku, naṭuvil narakattil oru paruvattait toṭaṅki muṭittuviṭṭu, mīti veḷiccaṅkaḷaip piṟaku eḻutiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu potuvākak karuta iṭamirukkiṟatu.

‘narakattil oruparuvam’ eṉṟa kavitait tokuti uḷaviyal pūrvamāṉa avaṉuṭaiya taṉvaralāṟu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām. piḷḷaip pirāyattilum, paḷḷipparuvattilum, aṭakku muṟaiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarccik kontaḷippum, taṉimaiyilum verlēṉuṭaṉum nakaraṅkaḷil poṟuppiṉṟic cuṟṟittirinta kālattil ēṟpaṭṭa kacappāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pātippāl aṭimaṉattil ēṟpaṭṭa atirvukaḷum mikanuṭpamāka ippāṭalkaḷil cittarikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. patiṉāṉku vayatuc ciṟuvaṉiṉ mutirntum mutirāta viṭalaik kaṉavukaḷum, etirkālattil veṭikkavirukkum avaṉatu pūkampak kavitaiyāṟṟalum, kaṭṭukkaḷai aṟuttuk koṇṭu pāyattuṭikkum avaṉ cutantira veṟiyum ikkavitait tokutiyil pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittokutikku rempō cūṭṭiya peyar ‘kataikaḷ’ (Stories)

innūliṉ pāyirattil caittāṉiṭattil avaṉ pēcuvatāka oru pakuti irukkiṟatu. atil, tāṉ teyvīkak kātalaik kaṇṭaṟiyavum, aḻakai ārātikkavum inta nūlai eḻutuvatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. appaṇikaḷaic ciṟanta muṟaiyil ceytu muṭikka narakattukkup payaṇam mēṟkoṇṭu, taṉ paritāpa muṭivait tāṉē tēṭik koṇṭa ōr āṉmāvaic cantittu, ataṉ uḷḷat tiruṭṭaiyum, valippup pitaṟṟalkaḷaiyum aṟintu koḷḷa virumpuvatākak kūṟukiṟāṉ. antap paritāpa āṉmā vēṟu yārumillai; ivaṉētāṉ, ippayaṇam iṟappiṟkup piṟaku mēṟkoḷvatu pōṉṟa oru kaṟpaṉai.

‘valippup pitaṟṟal’ eṉṟu avaṉ kuṟippiṭuvatu iravum pakalum avaṉ uḷḷattai ōyāmal varuttik koṇṭirukkum iru kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, oṉṟu verlēṉuṭaṉ koṇṭirunta takāta uṟavu; maṟṟoṉṟu 'col racavātam* (Verbal Alchemy) paṟṟiyatu. it tokutiyil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa kavitaikaḷil, cīrāṉa cantamō, etukai mōṉaiyō illai. etirpārāmal miṉṉum paṭimaṅkaḷai varicaiyāka aṭukkik kavitai viḷaivukaḷai rempō tōṟṟuvittirukkiṟāṉ.

innūliṉ iṟutiyil uḷḷa' ‘kālai’ (Morning), pirivu (Farewell), ākiya pāṭalkaḷil uḷḷattiṟku itamāṉa ōr iṉiya viṭiyalai avaṉ varavēṟṟup pāṭukiṟāṉ. “ivvaḷavu nāṭkaḷ eṉṉiṭam ‘nāṉ’ eṉpatu vēṟu ōr poruḷāka iruntatu. anta eṇṇattaik kaḷaintu viṭṭu, ‘nāṉ’ eṉṟa eṉ āṉmāvaiyum eṉ uṭalil ataṉ iruppaiyum uṇarntu koṇṭēṉ. ituvē nāṉ kaṇṭaṟinta aḻakuk kaṉavu; ituvē eṉ veṟṟi” eṉṟu uṇarcci vacappaṭṭup pāṭukiṟāṉ.

maṟṟoru tokuppāṉa ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷai’ verlēṉ vaṇṇattakaṭukaḷ (Coloured Plates) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār rempōviṉ vāḻkkaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa tuṉpaṅkaḷukkum kuḻappaṅkaḷukkum, vakkiraṅkaḷukkum tappi 'veḷiccaṅkaḷ ilakkiyavātikaḷiṉ kaikaḷil iṉṟum oḷikuṉṟāmal iruppataṟku avaṟṟiṉ teḷivum, poruḷamaitiyum, puriyumpaṭiyāṉa maṟaiporuḷ uttiyum kāraṇaṅkaḷākum. ittokuppil uḷḷa kavitaikaḷiṉ talaippukaḷ orē collāka amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavai paṉmaic coṟkaḷ ittalaippukaḷ mikavum kavarcciyāṉavai, kavitaiyiṉ ovvoru variyum paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ karuvūlam; avai kuṟippiṭum iṭaṅkaḷum mayaṅkavaikkum poyt tōṟṟaṅkaḷ.

Verbal alchemy: I flattered myself that I have invented a poetic language accessible, some day or other to at; the senses I adopted the most absurd and exaggerated more of expression conceivable...That is all past. I now know how to salute beauty-Rimbaud.

ittokuppil uḷḷa ellāk kavitaikaḷumē ciṟantavai eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. āṉāl kaṭaici mūṉṟu veḷiccaṅkaḷum mikavum oḷimikkavai. avaṟṟuḷ mētai (Genie) eṉṟa talaippil kūṟappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ, rempōviṉ paṭaippukkaḷil ātikkam celuttum kātal uṇarviṉ tiṟavukōlāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

“eṉṉāl kātalaic cāḷarattukku veḷiyē tūkki eṟiya muṭiyātu” eṉṟum “nāṉ kātalicaiyiṉ mūlak kuṟippaik kaṇṭaṟinta kalaiñaṉ” eṉṟum rempō perumitamāka veḷiccaṅkaḷil ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ.

rempō tāṉ kaṇṭaṟintatākak kuṟippiṭum ‘kātalicaiyiṉ mūlakkuṟippu’[4] etuvāka irukka muṭiyum eṉṟu tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ mūḷaiyaik kuḻappik koṇṭaṉar. vāḻkkai muḻuvatum nāttikaṉāka vāḻnta rempō, taṉ ceyta kuṟṟaṅkaḷiliruntu mīṭcipeṟa oru vaḻikāṭṭiyait tēṭiṉāṉ. anta vaḻikāṭṭiyait tēvaṉ eṉṟō, tēvamakaṉ eṉṟō, mecaiyyā eṉṟō, napi eṉṟō kuṟippiṭavillai. aṉpiṉ vaṭivamāka avaṉaik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ:

"avaṉ nammai aṟivāṉ; avaṉ nam ellāraiyum virumpukiṟāṉ. intak kuḷirkāla iravil, nilattiṉ oru kōṭiyiliruntu maṟṟoru kōṭivarai, pūmiyiṉ kontaḷikkum muṉaikaḷiliruntu kōṭṭai kottaḷam varai, nericalāṉa kuṭiyiruppiliruntu amaitiyāṉa kaṭaṟkarai varai, pārvaikku eṭṭiya tūram varai, valimaikkum vāṭṭattiṟkumiṭaiyil kūṭiyiruntu avaṉait taricikkavum, avaṉai mīṇṭum vaḻiyaṉuppavum vēṇṭum. alaikaḷiṉ naṭuvilum, paranta paṉippālaiyiṉ ucciyilum avaṉ uṭalaiyum, uyir mūccaiyum, avaṉ ñāṉap pēroḷiyaiyum piṉ paṟṟic cella vēṇṭum!"</poem>

perumpālāṉa kaviñarkaḷ kaṟpaṉaiyulakil cañcarittup paḻakkappaṭṭavarkaḷ. naṭaimuṟai ulakiṉ veḷippaṭaiyāṉa uṇmaikaḷ mikavum kacappāṉavai. avaṟṟaik kaviñarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷḷattayaṅkuvatōṭu, avaṟṟiliruntu tappiyōṭavum virumpukiṉṟaṉar. naṭaimuṟai ulakiliruntu tappic cella, mutalil tammai maṟakka vēṇṭum. tammai maṟakkak kaviñarkaḷ matu, apiṉ, kañcā pōṉṟa paḻakkaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. appaḻakkaṅkaḷ uḷakkaṭṭuppāṭṭaittaḷartti, atai ataṉ viruppappaṭi celuttukiṉṟaṉa. uḷḷam virumpum kaṟpaṉai ulakamoṉṟait tāmē paṭaittuk koṇṭu, ivvulakat tuṉpaṅkaḷai maṟantu tam viruppam pōl kaviñarkaḷ atil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. itai ‘maruḷmayakka nilai’ eṉpar. pōtalēraip pōlavē rempōvum oru kaṟpaṉaic corkkattaip paṭaittuk koṇṭu, atil payaṇam ceykiṟāṉ.

rempōviṉ paṭaippukkaḷil kuṭipōtaip paṭaku[5] (The Drunken Boat) eṉṟa kavitai mikavum viḷamparam peṟṟtu. itu oru kuṟiyīṭṭuk kavitai. iḷamaiyil tāyiṉ kaṇṭippālum aṭakku muṟaiyālum ēṟpaṭṭa kumuṟalkaḷum, verlēṉ uṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭa maṉavikāraṅkaḷum, nakarattiṉ iruṇṭa vāḻkkaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa tākkaṅkaḷum, vaṉmuṟaiyum āravāramum mikka peruṅkaṭalāka ikkavitaiyil uruvakappaṭuttappaṭukiṉaṟaṉa. akkaṭalil mitakkum oru tuṭuppillāta paṭaku rempōvukkuk kuṟiyīṭāka varukiṟatu. kaṭalil takkaipōl taḷḷāṭi varum ippaṭaku perumpuyalōṭum, kuṉṟaippōl kontaḷikkum alaiyōṭum paṉippāṟaiyōṭum, timiṅkilam kaṭaṟpāmpu pōṉṟa koṭiya kaṭal uyirikaḷōṭum pōrāṭi muṉṉēṟukiṟatu. iṉamaiyil kiṭaikkāta cutantiram intakkaṟpaṉaip payaṇattil rempōvukkuk kiṭaikkiṟatu. vīṭṭaiviṭṭu mūṉṟu muṟai ōṭiyapōtu, avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭa maṉakkōḷāṟāṉa makiḻcciyaiyum ippayaṇam paṭippavarkku uṇarttukiṟatu. ‘kuṭipōtaippaṭaku’ eḻutappaṭṭapōtu rempō kaṭalaiyē pārttatillai eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

pōtalēr kuṟiyīṭṭiyattiṉ (Symbolism) muṉṉōṭi, verlēṉ attuṟaiyil cātaṉaikaḷ nikaḻttiyavar. kirampō avaraiyum tāṇṭic ceṉṟavaṉ.

cempō mutalil kavitaiyai marapil eḻutiṉāṉ; iṭaiyil kaṭṭilāk kavitaikku vantāṉ; piṉṉar vacaṉattaiyē kavitaiyākkiṉāṉ. ‘pireñcuk kavitaiyaṉaittum etukai mōṉaiyamainta urainaṭai’ eṉṟu ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭṭāṉ.

camayam, camutāyam, araciyal ākiyavaṟṟai avaṉ eppaṭi etirttup puraṭciceytāṉō atēpōl kavitai naṭaiyaiyum etirttup puraṭci ceytāṉ. kavitaiccoṟkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa poruḷaiyē māṟṟi, coṟkaḷiṉ taṉṉiyakkam, colmayakkam, colviḷaiyāṭṭu, ōcai vēṟupāṭu ivaṟṟiṉmūlam moḻiyiṉ toṭakkanilaippaṇpukaḷaikkiḷappip paṭippavar uḷḷattil palvēṟu uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷait tōṟṟuvikka muyaṉṟāṉ. tāṉ veḷippaṭuttum ovvōr uṇarccikkum oru putiya kavitai moḻiyai uruvākka muṭiyum eṉṟu kaṉavu kaṇṭāṉ. ataṟkāka muyaṟciyum ceytāṉ. ‘kaṭaṟ pakkam’ eṉṟa kavitaiyil taṉ collāṟṟalāl nilapparappai nīrpparappākavum, nīrpparappai nilapparappākavum māṟṟik kāṇpikkiṟāṉ. intac ‘colracavāta’ vittaiyait toṭaṅkip piṉṉar kaiviṭṭu viṭṭāṉ.

“kaviñaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu kūṭātu, kavitai kaviñaṉuḷḷattil tāṉākak karuk koṇṭu, kaṟpaṉai vaṭivam peṟṟup pakkuvamaṭaintu poṅki varavēṇṭum. kaviñaṉ taṉatu uḷḷattil karuttukkaḷ malarntu varutalaik kaṇṭu cuvaittu, atai appaṭiyē aṉumatikka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu taṉatu pukaḻ peṟṟa kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāṉ rempō. ikkoḷkai avaṉaic carriyalicattukku iṭṭuc celkiṟatu, carriyalicattiṉ ūṟṟuk kaṇ rempō.

‘tīrkka taricaṉam’ vāykkap peṟṟavaṉ kaviñaṉ eṉṟār pōtalēr. tīrkkataricitāṉ (Seer) kaviñaṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ rempō, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭāta akak kāṭcikaḷ tīrkkataricikaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭum. inta akak kāṭci iṉpaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭut tammai maṟantiruppataṟkākavē cilar ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil maruḷ mayakka nilaiyai nāṭuvatuṇṭu. pōtalēr appaṭi maruḷ mayakka nilaiyil āḻntiruntu akak kaṭcikaḷai atītamākak kaṇṭavar,

pōtalēriṉ tīrkkataricaṉaṅkaḷai ēṟṟukkoṇṭa rempō avaruṭaiya kavitaiyiṉ vaṭivattai ēṟṟuk koḷḷavillai. ataippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “āviyulakaik kūviyaḻaittuk kāṇa muṭiyātavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu, kēṭka muṭiyātavaṟṟaik kēṭṭavarpōtalēr. avar oru tīrkkatarici! kaviyaracar! uṇmaiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ! āṉāl turatiruṣṭa vacamāka avar kalai nuṭpattilēyē karuttāka iruntār; avaruṭaiya ilakkiyam ciṟappāṉateṉṟu pukaḻappaṭṭālum, ataṉ vaṭivam mikac cātāraṇamāṉatu. karuttil putumai ceyyumpōtu vaṭivattilum putumai ceyya vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ rempō.

rempō kavitai ilakkaṇattaiyum, vaṭivattaiyum otukkit taḷḷiyatu pōla, coṟkaḷiṉ akarātip poruḷaiyum otukkiviṭṭu avaṉuṭaiya mutircci peṟṟa vacaṉa kavitaikaḷai eḻutiṉāṉ. avaṟṟil avaṉ kaṇṭa akakkāṭcikaḷai maṭṭum varicaip paṭuttiṉāṉ. ikkāṭcikaḷai iṇaikkum coṟṟoṭarkaḷaik kūṭa nīkki viṭṭāṉ. eṉṟālum avvacaṉaṅkaḷ kavitai nilaiyiliruntu tāḻntu pōkātataṟkuk kāraṇam, atil kūṟappaṭum akakkāṭcikaḷukku ēṟṟa puṟakkāṭcip paṭimaṅkaḷaik kalainuṭpattōṭu rempō kaiyāṇṭatutāṉ

rempōviṉ akak kāṭcikaḷ palatarappaṭṭa viparīta vaṭivaṅkaḷōṭu kuṭipōtaip paṭakil atīta kaṟpaṉaiyāka uruvakikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉmāviṉ uḷḷuṇarvuk kāṭcikaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ kūṭap paṭima uttiyāl viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉuṭaiya iḷamaikkāla niṉaivukaḷ avaṉ uḷḷattait taṭṭum pōtu kavitaikaḷ paṭaippāṟṟaliṉ uccattaiyē toṭukiṉṟaṉa.

nāṉ

ōyāmal aḻutēṉ.

uṇmai!

inta viṭiyalkaḷ eṉṉai

vētaṉaip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.



ovvoru nilavum

eṉakkut tuṉpam;

ovvoru cūriyaṉum

eṉakkuk kacappu.



kaṭu kaṭuppāṉa aṉpu

eṉṉaik

koñcaṅ koñcamāka

viḻuṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu.



eṉ muṭṭukkaḷ

muṟiyaṭṭum;

eṉ kappal

kaviḻantu pōkaṭṭum.



nāṉ karaiyēṟuvatu

ōr

airōppiyak kaṭaṟkaraiyāka

irukkaṭṭum,



aṅkē

kariya kuḷattil

mālai maṇattil

oru kuḻantai

mēmātap

paṭṭup pūcciyaip pōl

melliya paṭakuviṭṭut

taṉ kavalaikaḷaik

kuttuk kāliṭṭuk

kaḻippataik kāṇavēṇṭum





[kuṭipōtaippaṭaku]

mika iḷam vayatil aparimitamāṉa kavitai āṟṟal peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya rempō, taṉ pattoṉpatām vayatil kavitai eḻutuvatai ēṉ niṟutti viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu ellārukkum periya putir. avaṉ cantarppa cūḻnilaiyālō, etir pārāmalō kaviñaṉ āṉavaṉ allaṉ; ilakkiyat tuṟaiyil periya cātaṉaikaḷaic ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷōṭu kaviñaṉ āṉavaṉ; ataṟkākak kaṭumaiyāka uḻaittavaṉ; kalai vāḻkkaikkum naṭaimuṟai vāḻkkaikkum vēṟupāṭu illai eṉṟu karutit taṉ kavitaip paṇiyait toṭaṅkiyavaṉ. āṉāl uṇmai vēṟu vitamāka iruntatu.

taṉ kavitai nempukōlāl ulakaiyē puraṭṭi viṭalām eṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa rempōvukku naṭaimuṟai vāḻkkaiyil ēṟpaṭṭa cikkalkaḷum, ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷum tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi iruntaṉa. ataṉāl tāṉ taṉatu ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷaik’ kaṭaici viṟpaṉai (Clearance Sale) eṉṟa kavitaiyōṭu muṭittuk koḷkiṟāṉ. taṉ kaṭaici ilakkiya carakkai viṟṟuviṭṭuk kaṭaiyaiyum mūṭiviṭṭāṉ. kaṭai virittēṉ; koḷvārillai! eṉṟu vaṭalūr vaḷḷal varuntik kūṟiyatu ivaṉukkum poruntum.





↑ Pastoral theme

↑ kirēkkap purāṇattil varum ārḵpis, āṇṭavaṉ appōlōviṉ aruḷ peṟṟa pāṭakaṉ taṉ icaiyāl ellā uyiriṉaṅkaḷaiyum mayakka valla āṟṟal peṟṟavaṉ. avaṉ maṉaivi yūriṭis iṟanta pōtu avaḷai mīḷap peṟac cāvulakam celkiṟāṉ. cāvulakiṉ atitēvatai hēṭs. avaṉuṭaiya yāḻicaiyil mayaṅki, avaṉ maṉaiviyai oru nipantaṉaiyiṉ pēril aḻaittuc cella aṉumatikkiṟāṉ. iruḷ cūḻnta cāvulakai viṭṭu iruvarum veḷiyē ceṉṟa piṟakutāṉ ārḵpis taṉ maṉaiviyait tirumpip pārkka vēṇṭum eṉpatē nipantaṉai. ārḵpis cāvulakaiviṭṭu veḷiyil vantatum oḷi avaṉ mītu viḻuntatu: uṭaṉē avaṉ tirumpip pārttāṉ. appōtu yūriṭis cāvulakiṉ ellaikkuḷ iruntāṉ. hēṭsiṉ nipantaṉaiyai mīṟiya kāraṇattāl, ārḵpis taṉ maṉaiviyai mīṇṭum iḻantāṉ.

↑ pireñcil 'uyṉ cecōm āāmpēr’ eṉṟu uccarikkavēṇṭum.

↑ the key-signature of love

↑ pirañcup peyar Bateau lvre.

﻿ icai-

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu;

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu.





reyṉar mēriyā rilk

(1875–1926)



pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilum pālkaṉ nāṭukaḷil piṟanta oruvaṉ taṉṉai enta nāṭṭukkāraṉ eṉṟu collik koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi, aṅkē paṭaiyeṭuppukkaḷum, virainta āṭci māṟṟaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉa. kaviñar reyṉar mēriyā rilk pirākuvil piṟantavar; pokīmiya iṉattaic cārntavar; āstiriyak kuṭimakaṉ; jermaṉkavi.

tantai ōyvu peṟṟa āstiriya irāṇuva atikāri; peṇ kuḻantai vēṇṭum eṉṟu viruppattōṭu kāttirunta tāykku, rilk āṇākap piṟantatu perutta ēmāṟṟam: ēṉavē rilkkai oru peṇṇaip pōla vaḷarttāḷ ‘reṉi’ eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi. peṇṇuṭaiyum curuḷ muṭiyum aṇivittu, pommaic camaiyalaṟaiyoṉṟu viḷaiyāṭak koṭuttu vaḷarttāḷ. poruḷkaḷaiyum irukkaikaḷaiyum tuṭaittu tūymaiyāka vaittiruppatu eppaṭiyeṉṟum, vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷil tāykku eppaṭi utavuvatu eṉṟum kaṟṟuk koṭuttāḷ.

aṭikkaṭi nōyvāyppaṭum avaratu iḷamai irāṇuvap paḷḷiyilum, vaṇikak kallūriyilum kaḻintatu. pirāku palkalaikkaḻakattilum iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ caṭṭak kalvi payiṉṟār. koñcanāḷ vaḻakkuraiñarāka irunta taṉ uṟaviṉariṭam paṇipurintār. kaṭaiciyil ilakkiyap paṇiyē taṉakku ēṟṟa paṇiyeṉṟu muṭivu ceytār.



peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ maṇavilakkum, irāṇuvappaḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷum āvarai āḻnta tuyarattil āḻttiṉa. tamatu paṭukkaiyil amarntu cāvai vēṇṭi iṟaivaṉiṭam avar maṉṟāṭiya nēraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.

1899-1900 ām āṇṭukaḷil rilk lō āṇṭriyal calōmi eṉṟa peṇṇuṭaṉ ruciyappayaṇam mēṟkoṇṭār. aṅkē eḻuttāḷarum tattuvañāṉiyumāṉa liyō ṭālsṭāyaic cantittār. piṉṉar perliṉ tirumpiyatum ruciya nāṭṭu varalāṟṟaiyum, annāṭṭiṉ kalai ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum virumpip payiṉṟār. rilk, pirāṉs, skāṇṭi nēviyā, ittāli, speyiṉ, vaṭaāprikkā ākiya nāṭukaḷil cuṟṟup payaṇam mēṟkoṇṭālum, ruciyappayaṇam avar uḷḷattil periya tākkattai ēṟpaṭuttiyatu. avaruṭaiya etirkāla vaḷarccikku oru tūṇṭukōlākavum aṭippaṭaiyākavum viḷaṅkiyatu; tāṉ taricitta mutal puṉita pūmiyāka ataik karutiṉār. ruciyap payaṇattiṉ viḷaivākat tōṉṟiya kavitaikaḷil ruciya nāṭṭait taṉatu āṉmīkap peruveḷiyāka aticayaṅkaḷ mikka kaṟpaṉai ulakamāka-varuṇikkiṟār. 1900-il avar eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa ‘kaṭavuḷiṉ kataikaḷ’ (Stories of god) eṉṟa nūl avar uḷḷattil āṭci ceyta kaṟpaṉai ruciyāvai mika uyarvāka uruvakappaṭuttukiṟatu.

1902-il pukaḻpeṟṟa pireñcuc ciṟpiyāṉa rōṭiṉiṉ ceyalāḷarākac cērntu iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ paṇipurintār. piṉṉar pāricil pattāṇṭukaḷ taṅkiyiruntu tam ilakkiyap paṇiyait toṭarntār. 1915-ām āṇṭil irāṇuvap paṇikkāka aḻaikkappaṭṭu viyaṉṉā ceṉṟār. irāṇuvattiṉ kaṭumaiyāṉa naṭaimuṟaikaḷait tākkuppiṭikka muṭiyāmal nōy vāyppaṭṭār. avaruṭaiya mōcamāṉa uṭal nilai kāraṇamāka irāṇuvattil avarukku eḻuttar paṇi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ōriraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷil appaṇiyiliruntum viṭutalaipeṟṟu myūṉic ceṉṟu taṅkiṉār. tamatu kaṭaici nāṭkaḷai sviṭcarlāntil kaḻittār.

ṣelliyum pairaṉum kaviñarkaḷuḷ mikavum aḻakāṉavarkaḷ; ṭapiḷyū ec. āṭaṉ kaviñarkaḷuḷ mikavum aḻakaṟṟavar: aḷavaṟṟa kuṭiyāl cīrkulaintu, tōl curuṅki, oṭṭuk kantalāka avar mukam kāṭciyaḷikkum. rilkkiṉ camakālattu eḻuttāḷar oruvar rilkkiṉ tōṟṟattaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, 'aṭikkaṭi mīcaiyaic cuṇṭum, cuṇṭeli pōl kāṭciyaḷikkiṟār, eṉṟu vēṭikkaiyākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

mīcaiyōṭum, iṉiya uṟutiyāṉa mukattōṭum, mevitta tāṭaiyōṭum etirppaṭṭataik kirakikkum oḷi poruntiya kūrtta viḻikaḷōṭum, melliya nīḷamāṉa aḻakiya viralkaḷōṭum rilk paṭattil kāṭciyaḷikkiṟār. avar cirippai virumpātavar; jermāṉiyak kaṇṭippōṭu kūṭiya meyviḷakka vāti; paṭitta iḷaiñarkaḷōṭum paṇpaṭṭa iḷaṅ kalaiñarkaḷōṭum nīṇṭanāḷ kalanturaiyāṭik kaviñaṉiṉ kuṟikkōḷkaḷaik kaṟṟuṇarntavar.

ṣelli pireñcup puraṭci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻantai; rilk, taṉṉaittāṉē aḻittuk koḷḷappuṟappaṭṭa airōppiyap pōr veṟiyiṉ etirppuṇarcci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻantai.

ciṟpiyiliruntu cīmāṭṭivarai rilkkiṉ vāḻkkaiyil pala peṇkaḷ kuṟikkiṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṭam rilk koṇṭa kātaluṟavum cuvaiyāṉatu; putirāṉatu. rilkkiṉ mutaṟ kātal 1897-il vēlari ṭēviṭ rōṉḵpīlṭ eṉṟa peṇṇiṭam tuvakkaviḻāk koṇṭatu. avaratu mutal kavitai nūlukku avaḷ ūkkac caktiyāka viḷaṅkiyatōṭu anta nūlai veḷiyiṭum poruṭcelavaiyum avaḷē ēṟṟāḷ. ‘valli’ eṉṟu taṉ kuṟippukaḷil vāñcaiyuṭaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟār rilk. āṉāl avaḷ uṟavu kuṟukiya kālattil muṟintatu.

aṭuttu rilkkaik kavarntavaḷ ‘lō calōmi’. avaḷ atirccitarum aḻakut tēvatai. irupatu vayatil jermāṉiyat tattuvañāṉi nīṭcēyait taṉ aḻakiṉ muṉ maṇṭiyiṭac ceytavaḷ. rilkkaic cantitta pōtu avaḷukku vayatu muppattaintu; tirumaṇamāṉavaḷ. rilk appōtu irupattu mūṉṟu vayatuk kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷai. eṉṟālum taṉatu aṉupavakkaraṅkaḷālum amuta moḻikaḷālum rilkkai aravaṇaittut tālāṭṭiṉāḷ. kaṇṭatum kātal! viraivil avarkaḷ pirintālum, kātal toṭarpu aṟṟup pōkavillai; vāḻkkaiyiṉ iṟutivarai nīṭittatu. lō calōmi rilkkiṉ uḷḷattukku nerukkamāṉavaḷākavum uṇarccip parimāṟṟaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavaḷākavum, ātarca naṅkaiyākavum eppōtum viḷaṅkiṉāḷ.

avar neruṅkip paḻakiya peṇkaḷuḷ ṭirisṭi nakarukkarukil irunta ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭai iḷavaraci ‘mērivāṉtāṉ’ kuṟippiṭattakkavaḷ. avaḷuṭaiya aṉpukkum aravaṇaippukkum kaṭṭuppaṭṭu āṇṭuk kaṇakkāka avaḷuṭaiya kōṭṭaiyil taṅkiyiruntār rilk. pukaḻpeṟṟa ‘ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā’ (Duing Elegies) tōṉṟak kāraṇamāka irunta naṅkaiyum ivaḷē

ruciyap payaṇam muṭintu jermaṉ tirumpiyatum. cilk ‘vorpsvīṭ’ eṉṟa ciṟṟūrukkuc ceṉṟār. aṅkē ‘kiḷērā vesṭhāḵp’ eṉṟa iḷañ ciṟpiyaiccantittār. avaḷ pukaḻ peṟṟa ciṟpi rōṭiṉuṭaiya māṇavi. iruvarum kātalittu 1901-il tirumaṇm ceytu koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷukku ‘rūt’ eṉṟu kuḻantaiyum piṟantatu. kuṭumpu vāḻkkai eṉṉum kaṭṭuttaṟiyil rilk nilaiyākat taṅkum paḻakkamillai. avar empōtum payaṇam ceytu koṇṭē iruppār. avar mēyccal tarai periyatu.

kātalaippaṟṟi rilk koṇṭirunta karuttu putumaiyāṉatu. muḻumai peṟātatum, kaimmāṟu karutātatum tāṉ uṉṉatamāṉa kātal eṉpatu rilkkiṉ karuttu. ‘oru peṇṇiṉ tōḷmītu kaivaitta aḷavilēyē, eṉṉuṭaiya kātaliṉ muḻumaiyāṉa uṇarcciyaitteḷivāka avaḷukku uṇartta muṭiyum’ eṉṟu avar kuṟippiṭukiṟār. rilkkiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa ārvalarkaḷ ellārum peṇkaḷē. avarkaḷ avarai naṉku purintiruntārkaḷ; avariṭamiruntu avarkaḷ etaiyum etirpārkkavillai. eṉṟālum avariṭam paḻakiyapōtu taṅkaḷ kātal niṟaivēṟiya uḷḷa niṟaivu avarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭatu.

puṉaiviyak kaviñarāka arumpi, aḻakiyak kaviñarāka malarntu iruppiyak kaviñarāka maṇam parappi, patippiyak kaviñarākak kāyttu iṟutiyil tamatu conta uḷḷuṇarvukaḷukkum akak kāṭcikaḷukkum karuttu vaṭivam koṭukkum caṅkētak kaviñarākak kaṉintu viḷaṅkiṉār.

citaṟik kiṭakkum ciṟukavitaikaḷ niṟaiya eḻutik kuvittiruntālum, rilkkiṟku aḻiyāp pukaḻaic cērkkum kavitait tokutikaḷāka mūṉṟaik kuṟippiṭalām. avai ‘mālṭē lāriṭs pirikkiṉ kuṟippukaḷ’ (The Note books of Malte Lawrids Brigge) ‘ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷ’ Duino Eeegies ‘ārḵpicai nōkki eḻutiya īrēḻvarik kavitaikaḷ’ (Sonnets To Orpheus) eṉpaṉa. ivai nikaḻ kālattaip piṉṉukkut taḷḷiya muṉṉōṭik kavitaikaḷ.

mālṭē lāriṭs pirik, rilkkiṉ paṇpukaḷai appaṭiyē uḷ vāṅkik koṇṭa kaṟpaṉaip pāttiram. mēlum pōtalēriṉ uruvākkattaiyum ippāttirap paṭaippil kāṇalām. ikkuṟippukaḷil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitaikaḷ rilkkiṉ paṭaippāṟṟalukkuc cavālāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantaip paruvattaip paṟṟiyum, kuṟikkōḷ illāta kātalaip paṟṟiyum, cāvaip paṟṟiyum, tuṟavipōl naṭikkum peṇkaḷaip paṟṟiyum rilk nūtaṉamāka karuttukkaḷai mālṭē kuṟippukkaḷil veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟār

ikkuṟippukaḷuḷ ‘mukaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟa pakutiyil, ovvoru maṉitaṉum pala mukaṅkaḷaip peṟṟiruppatākavum, oru camayattil oṉṟai maṭṭum avaṉ aṇintu koṇṭu maṟṟavaṟṟai iruppil vaittiruppatākavum cilar aṭikkaṭiyum avaciyam ēṟpaṭum pōtum tamatu mukaṅkaḷai māṟṟik koḷvatākavum cuvaiṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāris nakaril nāṭṭarṭām terumuṉaiyil, taṉatu mukattaik kaiyil eṭuttu vaittukkoṇṭu amarntirunta peṇṇaic cantittatāka oru mikaikkaṟpaṉaiyai cilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār:

“teru veṟiccōṭippōy amaitiyāka iruntatu; anta veṟumai eṉakkuc calippāka iruntatu; cōrvōṭu atteruveṅkum cuṟṟit tirintēṉ. eṉ kālkaḷ marakkaṭṭaikaḷākak kaṉattaṉa, oru peṇ taṉ kaikaḷai mukattukkut tāṅkalāka vaittuk kuṉintu koṇṭiruntāḷ: eṉṉaik kaṇṭatum tiṭukkiṭṭu veṭukkeṉa nimirntāḷ, appōtu avaḷ mukam kaiyōṭu vantu viṭṭatu, avaḷ kaiyil kiṭanta uṭkavinta veṟṟu mukattaip pārttu nāṉ atircciyaṭaintēṉ. ataiviṭa atu peyarttu eṭukkappaṭṭa iṭam, mukam illāmal kāttiruppataik kāṇa añciṉēṉ.”

mēlē kuṟippiṭuvatu pōṉṟa kāṭcikaḷai aṭimaṉak kōṭpāṭṭu ōviyarkaḷiṉ paṭaippiltāṉ kāṇa muṭiyum. ōviyar, rōṭiṉiṭam cilakālam ceyalāḷarāka iruntiruntālum, itupōṉṟa ōviyaṅkaḷaip pārttirukka muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, rōṭiṉ cevviya ōviyar (Classicist). kirēkkat teyvamāṉa appōlōviṉ cilaiyaip pārttu uṇarccivacappaṭṭu rilk pāṭiya kavitai yoṉṟil,

itu-

uṉṉaip pārkka virumpāta iṭam.

uṉakku iṅkē iṭamillai;

nī uṉṉai māṟṟik koḷ.





eṉṟa varikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. rilkkiṉ inta varikaḷai ilakkiya vātikaḷ aṭikkaṭi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāḷvatuṇṭu. inta varikaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷē mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa mikaik kaṟpaṉaiyāka viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭaiyil oru nāḷ taṉimaiyil amarntirunta pōtu, tam uḷḷattil nikaḻtta kavitai āvēcattaik kīḻk kaṇṭavāṟu paṭarkkaiyil pativu ceykiṟār rilk.

“iṉam puriyātoṉṟu avaṉiṭam aṉṟu nikaḻntatu.vaḻakkam pōl oru puttakattaik kaiyilēntiya vaṇṇam aṅku miṅkumāka naṭanta koṇṭirunta avaṉ, kavaiyākap pirintirunta marakkiḷai. yoṉṟil vacatiyāka amarntu koṇṭāṉ. mikavum ōyvāka amarnta nilaiyil kaiyilirunta puttakattai mūṭivaittu viṭṭuccuṟṟiyirunta amaitiyāṉa, aḻakiya iyaṟkaic cūḻnilaiyil taṉṉai maṟantu mūḻkiṉāṉ. itaṟku muṉ aṉupavittirāta ōr uṇarcci veḷḷam avaṉuḷ alaiyākap paravuvatai uṇarntāṉ, avaṉ amarntirunta marattiṉ naṭuviliruntu avvuṇarcci melliya atirvukaḷāka avaṉuḷ iṟaṅkiyatu. itupōṉṟa iṉpa atircciyai avaṉ eṉṟum aṉupavittatillai. avaṉ uṭampē āṉmāvāka māṟi, iyalpāṉa taṉ ceyalkaḷai maṟantu, ētō oru pērāṟṟalait taṉṉuḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu pōliruntatu, atuvumaṉṟi eḷitil pulappaṭāta iyaṟkaiyiṉ maṟupuṟattaik kaṇṭatupōlavum iruntatu.”

rilk ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭaiyil iruntapōtu tammai yārō aḻaippataippōṉṟu uṇarntār. ‘nāṉ kūviyaḻaittāl, tēvataikaḷiṉ kūṭṭattil kēṭpavar yār?’ eṉṟu akkural aṭikkaṭi kūṟikkoṇṭiruntatu. anta aṭiyē ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpāviṉ tuvakkamāka amaintatu.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷai rilk 1912- ām āṇṭil eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉār. āṉāl avaṟṟai 1923 varai muṭikkavillai. ataṟkup palakāraṇaṅkaḷai āyvāḷarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. 1912-ām āṇṭil avaruṭaiya karuttukkaḷaiyum, kavitaippāṇiyaiyum tīviramākap pātikkum aṉupavam avarukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. uvamaikaḷ mūlam karuttu viḷakkam ceyvataik koñcam koñcamākak kaiviṭṭu, karuttaiyum karuttu viḷakkattaiyum oṉṟākap purintu koḷḷum uruvaka uttikku māṟikkoṇṭiruntarar; itu mutaṟ kāraṇam. avaruḷḷattil patitta uṇarvukaḷ karutturuvam peṟa nīṇṭa cintaṉai tēvaippaṭṭatu: itu iraṇṭāvatu kāraṇam. mēlum, ivviraṅkaṟpākkaḷai eḻutum pōtu uṇarcci pūrvamāṉa ilakkiyat tūṇṭutal tēvaippaṭṭatu. attuṇṭutalaic ceyaṟkaiyākap peṟamuṭiyātu; itu mūṉṟāvatu kāraṇam.

ivviraṅkaṟpākkaḷ rilkkiṉ apūrvap paṭaippāṟṟalukkuc cāṉṟāka uḷḷaṉa. ippākkaḷum paṭippōrai mayakkavaikkum maṟaiporuḷkaḷai uḷḷaṭakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ituvē ippākkaḷiṉ valimaiyum, toymaiyum ākum. āṅkilak kavitaiyulakil, ṭapiḷyū. pi. yēṭcum, ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭum vakikkum iṭattai jermāṉiyak kavitai ulakil rilk vakikkiṟār. ṭi. es. eliyaṭ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'pāḻnilattiṟku' oppāka rilkkiṉ ‘ṭiyūṉō icaṅkaṟpākkaḷai’, ilakkiyavātikaḷ matikkiṉṟaṉar.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷiṉ veṟṟikku rilk payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkum kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitaiyāppē kāraṇam eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu yāppu muṟaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruntāl, rilkkiṟkut tēvaippaṭṭa nikaḻcci kiṭaikkāmal pōyirukkum. rilk ivaṟṟuḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkum mūvacaic cīrkaḷ, kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitai yāppil vaḻakkamākak kāṇappaṭum cantat taṭaikaḷaip pōkkiviṭṭaṉa.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷiṉ taṉippāvaiyō, ataṉ pakutiyaiyō tammait tavira vēṟu yārum patippikkak kūṭātu eṉṟu rilk aṟivittiruntār. iraṅkaṟpā muḻuvataiyum vēṟu oruvar patippikkavum muṭiyātu. patippikkak kūṭiya kavitai amaippilum avai illai.

ārḵpicai nōkki eḻutappaṭṭa īrēḻvarip pākkaḷ mottam aimpattaintu. ivai aṟputaṅkaḷ nikaḻttiya kirēkkappāṭakaṉāka ārḵpiciṉ āṟṟalōṭu eḻutappaṭṭavai. ārḵpiciṉ vāḻkkaiyil kēṭpōrai mayaṅka vaikkum aṟputaṅkaḷ iruppatu pōla, intap pākkaḷilum paṭippōrai mayaṅka vaikkum maṟai poruḷkaḷ niṟaiya uṇṭu.

"ippākkaḷ iṟukkamum curukkamum mikkavai; ivaṟṟai ‘aṟintu koḷḷutal’ eṉpataiviṭa ‘uḷḷuṇarvāl uṇarntu koḷḷutalē’ paṭippōrāl iyalum” eṉṟu rilkkē ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ikkuṟippu rempōviṉ kavitaikaḷukkum, avaṟṟaiyoṭṭip piṉṉāl tōṉṟiya kavitaikaḷukkum poruntum eṉṟālum, ippākkaḷil kaiyāḷappaṭṭuḷḷa uttikaḷ rilk kaippaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷap peritum utavukiṉṟaṉa.

kirēkka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mītum kalaikaḷiṉ mītum rilkkiṟku niṟaiya īṭupāṭu uṇṭu. kirēkkap pāṭakaṉāṉa ārḵpiciṉ icaiyāṟṟal, avaṉ maṉaivi yūriṭisiṉ iṟappu, avaḷai mīṭka avaṉ iṟappulakam ceṉṟu mīḷutal, kaṭaiciyil am muyaṟciyil ēṟpaṭṭa avalam yāvum mikavum uṇarccikaramākak kaṟpaṉainayattōṭu ippākkaḷil pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai kirēkkat teyvattai nōkkip pāṭappaṭṭatāka amaintiruntālum, uṇmaiyāka makkaḷai nōkkip pāṭappaṭṭavai. vāḻkkaiyil ēṟpaṭum iḻappukkaḷai eppaṭit tāṅkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum, avaṟṟai eppaṭi makiḻcciyāka māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum maṟai poruḷākap pāṭukiṟār rilk.

rilk icaiyai mikavum virumpiṉār. ārḵpiciṉ yāḻum. avaṉ icaiyum rilkkiṉ neñcil nīṅkāta iṭattaip peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. ‘icaiyē ulakam; icaiyē vāḻkkai. ulakiṉ ellāk kalaikaḷum icaiyiṉ māṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷē’ eṉpatu rilkkiṉ karuttu. icaiyaip paṟṟit tam kavitaiyil ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭumpōtu,

icai-

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu

itayattiṉ veḷivaḷarcci:



pēccu mauṉittu

avalap pātaiyil

cellum pōtu

iṭaiyil eḻum

ceṅkuttuc cuvar,



itu-

uṇarvukaḷai

ōcai nilamāka ākkuvatu.





[tuṇṭup pāṭal-

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā]

mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa mūṉṟu nūlkaḷ allāmal ‘putuk kavitaikaḷ’ (The New Poems) eṉṟa nūlaiyum rilkkiṉ paṭaippukaḷuḷ mukkiyamāṉa oṉṟākak kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum. itil uḷḷa kavitaikaḷ iraṇṭu tokuppukaḷāka muṟaiyē 1907, 1908 ām āṇṭukaḷil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟuttai (The Panther) piyāṉō payiṟci (Piano Practice), līṭā (Leda) veṉicil kālaṅ kaṭanta ilaiyutirk kālam (Late Autumn In Venice), spāṉiya naṭaṉamaṅkai (Spanish Dancer) ūtārip piḷḷaiyiṉ puṟappāṭu ( The Departure of The Prodigal Son) eṉṟa pukaḻ peṟṟa kavitaikaḷ ittokuppukkaḷilēyē uḷḷaṉa. mutalil kuṟippiṭṭa kavitaikaḷukkum, intap putuk kavitaikaḷukkum aṭippaṭaiyil oru vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. ikkavitaikaḷil pārppavariṉ uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷaip paṟṟi atikam cintikkāmal, pārkkappaṭum poruḷiṉ tōṟṟam, taṉmaip paṟṟiyē rilk atikamākac cintikkiṟār.

rilkkiṉ kavitaikaḷaik kūrntu kavaṉittāl, avaruṭaiya paṭippaṭiyāṉa vaḷarcciyum, avar eppaṭip paṭṭavar eṉpatum pulaṉākum. rilk iḷamaiyiliruntē taṉimai viruppamuḷḷavar; etaiyum kūrntu pārkkum paḻakkam uṭaiyavar; mālṭē lāriṭs pirik kuṟippukaḷil ‘taṉ muṉaippumikka maṉitar’ (obstinate man) oruvaraip paṟṟiya kuṟippu ōriṭattil varukiṟatu. ammaṉitar nāṭaka āciriyar ipcaṉ eṉṟum, ōviyar rōṭiṉ eṉṟum āyvāḷarkaḷ iruvēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. anta maṉitariṭamiruntē ‘tīviramākak kūrntupār’ eṉṟa pāṭattait tāmkaṟṟuk koṇṭatāka eḻutukiṟār rilk.

ciṟpi rōṭiṉ mikavum kaṇṭippāṉavar: yāriṭamum eḷitil ottuppōkātavar. avariṭattil ceyalāḷarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya rilk‘ avariṭamiruntu tam uḷḷoḷiyāl oru kavitaippāṇiyaik kaṟṟuṇarntār. akkavitaikkuk karup poruḷ kiṭaiyātu. kaviñaṉ āḻnta taṉimaiyil irukkum pōtu, atu taṉakkuriya karuvait tāṉē paṭaittu veṭṭa veḷiyil eṭuttu niṟutta vēṇṭum. ataik karukkavitai (Poems of Things) eṉṟu rilk kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ṭapiḷyū. ec. āṭaṉ ataip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu.

vāḻka rilkkiṉ karukkavitai

atu taṉimaiyiṉ cāntā kḷās![1]





eṉṟu pārāṭṭukiṟār.

rilkkiṉ moḻipeyarppāḷar oruvar avaraip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “rilk tamatu nēraṅkaḷaip poruṭkāṭcic cālaikaḷilum, nūlakaṅkaḷilum pūṅkākkaḷilum kaḻippār. iravu nēraṅkaḷil vītikaḷilum, mantai veḷikaḷilum, cīṉ natip pālattiṉ mītum taṉimaiyil cintaṉaiyōṭu cuṟṟit tirivār” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

taṉimai rilkkiṟkup piriyamāṉa oṉṟu. taṉimaiyai avar uṇarntatu maṭṭumallāmal ataip pōṟṟic cuvaittār; mātākōvil toḻukaiyiṉpōtu amaitiyāka eḻuppappaṭum iṉiya icaiyāka ataik karutiṉār. taṉimai taṉṉai vāṭṭuvatāka iḷaṅkaviñar oruvar rilkkiṟku eḻutiya pōtu, taṉimai- uḷḷārnta paranta taṉimaitāṉ kaviñaṉukku vēṇṭum. namakkuḷḷēyē nām, nīṇṭanēram oruvaraiyum etirppaṭāmal taṉimaiyil naṭakka vēṇṭum. atutāṉ nām aṭaiya vēṇṭiya iṉpattiṉ ellai. taṉimai, kuḻantaiyiṉ taṉimaiyaip pōl irukka vēṇṭum. kuḻantai, taṉakkuḷḷiruntu veḷiyulutaip pārppatu pōl, kaviñaṉ taṉ uḷḷattiṉ āḻattiliruntu, taṉimaiyiṉ utaviyōṭu veḷiyulakaip pārkka vēṇṭum. atu tāṉ kaviñaṉiṉ paṇi, taram, toḻil” eṉṟu avaṉukku aṟivurai vaḻaṅkukiṟār.

evaṉ uṟutiyāka naṭuvil nilaittirukkiṟāṉō, avaṉē vīraṉ’ eṉṟa emarcaṉiṉ poṉmoḻi rilkkiṟku mikavum piṭittamāṉa oṉṟu. rilk ōyāmal uḻaittār. tuṉpattukku ilakkāṉa tam toṭakka nilaiyiliruntu, paṭaippāṟṟalai iṭaiviṭātu veḷippaṭuttum tēvataiyākat tammai uyarttik koṇṭār.

avar piṟappāl kattōlikka kiṟittuva camayattaiccārntavarāka iruntālum, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘kiṟistuviṉ akak kāṭcikaḷ’ (Vision of Christ) eṉṟa nūlil, iyēcuvai oru ñāṉiyāka ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārē tavira avatāra puruṣarāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷavillai. 1898-il ṭaskaṉi nakaraippārttu viṭṭut tirumpiyavuṭaṉ tamatu kuṟippukkaḷil oru karuttait teriyappaṭuttukiṟār. atil tam uḷḷattil nilaipēṟu koṇṭu tammai naṭattic cellum ōru pērāṟṟalait tām uṇarvatākavum, ataittavira vēṟu teyvam, etuvum iruppatākattām nampavillai eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār. veḷiyil kāṇappaṭum maraṅkaḷ, malaikaḷ, mēkaṅkaḷ; alaikaḷ yāvum tām uḷḷattil kāṇum uṇmaikaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷē eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār. oḻukkak kēṭāṉa navanākarika camutāyattaiyum, kōvilukku vārantōṟum cellum carācari paktaṉiṉ āravārattaiyum, vacatikku ēṟṟapaṭi vaṭivamaikkappaṭṭa camayattiṉ (Ready-made religion) veṟumaiyaiyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭa vanta rilk “ñāyiṟṟukkiḻamai añcalakaṅkaḷaip pōlat tūymaiyākavum, mūṭiyum, ēmāṟṟam taruvatākavum uḷḷatu” eṉṟu uḷḷam naintu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.

tamatu ciṟuttaip pāṭṭil ‘maiyattaic cuṟṟi naṭaṉamiṭum pērāṟṟal’ (The dance of forces round a centre) eṉṟa oru karuttaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. appērāṟṟal naṭam puriyum maiyam tamatu itayamē eṉpatu avar karuttu anta maiyam tamatu itayattil nīṅkāmal iṭam peṟavēṇṭu meṉṟāl, atu tam akak kāṭcikaḷaip puṟak kāṭcikaḷāka iṭaiyaṟāmal māṟṟi veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tamatu neruṅkiya tōḻiyāṉa lō calōmikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil “nāṉ kalaiyai vāḻkkaiyiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttip pārkka virumpavillai; eppaṭiyum avaiyiraṇṭiṟkum poruḷ oṉṟākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tuvakka kālattil kalai, vāḻkkai ākiya iraṇṭiṉ ellaikaḷai varaiyaṟuttu, vāḻkkai aṉupavaṅkaḷ mūlamākak kalaiyai vaḷappaṭuttiṉār rilk. tamatu tōḻiyum iḷam oviyarumāṉa. pavlā mōtar ṣōṉ pekkar’ eṉpavar pēṟu kālattil uyir tuṟantapōtum, tammaip pōl kavitaiyāṟṟalum cintaṉai vaḷamum mikka ‘keḷaṇṭ vulpkirāḵp varṉklkrīt’ eṉṟa iḷaṅkaviñar taṟkolai ceytu koṇṭa pōtum appēriṭikaḷai rilkkāl tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyavillai. avarkaḷ cāvukkāka iṟaṅki eḻutappaṭṭa icaip pāṭalkaḷil iraṅkalaiyum pukaḻcciyaiyum oṉṟu paṭuttiyiruppatōṭu, vāḻvaiyum cāvaiyum ārpica nōkkil iṇaittu muṭittirukkiṟār. “yār veṟṟiyaippaṟṟippēcukiṟārkaḷ? etaiyum tāṅkum itayamē vāḻkkai” eṉṟu kūṟip peru mūccu viṭukiṟār rilk.

potuvākak kaviñarkaḷ ulakil uḷḷa parup poruḷkaḷaip pārttu uṇarcci vacappaṭṭu ataṟkēṟpa uḷḷuṇarvukaḷai veḷippaṭuttuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl rilkkiṉ kavitaiyāṟṟal ataṟku nērmāṟāṉatu. avaratu āṉmīka uḷḷam eppōtum akk kāṭcikaḷilēyē īṭupaṭṭirukkum. kuṟippāka ulakat tuṉpaṅkaḷāl pātikkappaṭṭu varuntik kalaiyiṭam aṭaikkalam pukumpōtu avaruḷḷattil akak kāṭcikaḷ niṟaiyat tōṉṟum akkāṭcikaḷ veḷippaṭuttum uḷḷuṇarvukaḷukku oppāṉa puṟak kāṭcikaḷai veḷiyulakil tēṭuvār. piṟaku irṇṭaiyum pirikka muṭiyātapaṭi oṉṟu paṭuttuvār. puṟavulakil kāṇappaṭum acaiyāp poruḷkaḷ kūṭa, avaruḷḷat tuṇarccikaḷōṭu uṟavāṭum uyiriṉaṅkaḷākat teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

vīṭu pāttiram ōṭai katavu

palakaṇi pālam paḻamaram eṉṟu

veṟumaiyāyk kuṟippiṭukiṟōm

nāṉ kūṟukiṟēṉa

purintu koḷḷuṅkaḷ.

ip poruḷkaḷ

eṉakku-

evvaḷavu nerukkamāṉavai eṉṟu

avaikaḷē niṉaittirukka muṭiyātu.





[ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā]

‘kaviñaṉ’ eṉṟa talaippil eḻutiya kavitaiyil, tamatu akak kāṭcikaḷ pulappaṭuttiya nuṇṇuṇarvukaḷai ārḵpicōṭu toṭarpupaṭutti kaṉavu pōl cittarikkiṟār rilk.

kaṭavuḷ āṟṟalmikkavar

cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ

teyvīka yāḻicaiyait

toṭarntu vara muṭiyumā?



avaṉ aṟivu

kuḻampip pōyirukkiṟatu.

mēlum-

itayap pātaiyiṉ kuṟukkē

appōlōviṉ

kōvil kiṭaiyātu

pāṭutal

oru viruppamō

allatu

eḷitil kiṭṭum

kātaṟ cantippō aṉṟu.





pāṭutal-

kaṭavuḷukku eḷiya ceyal

āṉāl namakku..?

maṇṇil irukkum nammai

avaṉ eppōtu

viṇṇuk kuyarttuvāṉ?



iḷaiñaṉē!

mutaṟ kātal

poṅkik kiḷampum pōtu

itaḻk katavai uṭaittuk koṇṭu

uṇarcci

ōcaiyuruvam peṟukiṟatē!

atuvaṉṟu pāṭṭu,



pāṭuvatai

maṟakkak kaṟṟuk koḷ;

atu tēvaiyumillai.

uṇmaiyāṉa pāṭṭukku

oru putu mātiriyāṉa

cuvācam tēvai.

ōr amaiti!

oru kaṭavuḷiṉ cilirppu!

oru meṉ kāṟṟu!





[ārḵpicai nōkki]

1922 ām āṇṭil mārṣal prōsṭ eṉṟa kaviñaṉ iṟantapōtu avaṉaip paṟṟi rilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu tam kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭṭeḻutiyuḷḷār:

“ivaṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya kavitai utti mikaccemmaiyāṉatu. itu entakkuṟippiṭṭa poruḷilum ūṉṟi niṟpatillai. itu viḷaiyāṭṭāka veḷippaṭuttum karuttukkaḷ īṭaṟṟa tulliyattōṭu, putiya putirkaḷai mēṉmēlum tōṟṟuvitta vaṇṇam aṅkumiṅkum

oṭṭikkoṇṭu ūcalāṭum.”
